Abstract: We present our submission to the LLM track of the 2026 Computational Models of Reference, Anaphora and Coreference (CRAC 2026) shared task. With an average CoNLL F1 score of 74.32 on the official test set, our system ranked first in the LLM track, and third overall. Our system is based on the Gemma-3-27b model, fine-tuned using a two-stage strategy with a multilingual base adapter followed by dataset-specific adapters. We represent mention spans by their headword using an XML-inspired format with local reindexing and annotate documents iteratively. These design choices proved effective across languages, document lengths, and annotation guidelines.

</details>


### [19] [Skills on the Fly: Test-Time Adaptive Skill Synthesis for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16986)
*Jingxing Wang,Chenyu Zhou,Zhihui Fu,Jun Wang,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang,Jianghao Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillTTA，一种测试时自适应技能合成方法，通过检索与当前任务相关的少量训练轨迹并合成为临时的任务特定文本技能，在不更新模型参数的情况下实现测试时自适应。在SpreadsheetBench、ALFWorld和BigCodeBench上的实验表明，该方法显著优于静态技能合成基线，并匹配甚至超越了更复杂的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体受益于可复用的技能库，但测试时任务通常需要比静态技能库所能提供的更具体的指导。现有方法难以在测试阶段动态调整技能以适应具体任务需求。

Method: SkillTTA方法包含两个阶段：首先检索与当前测试任务相关的少量训练轨迹（包括成功和失败的轨迹），然后将这些轨迹合成为一个临时的、任务特定的文本技能。求解器模型保持固定，适应完全通过生成的上下文实现，而非参数更新。

Result: 在SpreadsheetBench上，Pass@1从0.397提升到0.505；在BigCodeBench上，Pass@1从0.517提升到0.651；在ALFWorld上，与更强的内存学习基线相差4个百分点以内，且生成了报告方法中最短的成功轨迹。

Conclusion: 合成的技能优于原始轨迹提示；top-k检索应保持较小值；失败的轨迹特别有用，因为它们暴露了重复出现的评估器常见错误。

Abstract: LLM agents benefit from reusable skills, yet test-time tasks often require guidance more specific than a static skill library can provide. We propose \emph{SkillTTA}, a Test-Time Adaptive Skill Synthesis method that retrieves a small set of training trajectories relevant to the current task and synthesizes them into a temporary, task-specific textual skill. The solver model is kept fixed, so adaptation happens entirely through generated context rather than parameter updates. We evaluate the method on SpreadsheetBench, ALFWorld, and BigCodeBench. Compared with static trajectory-to-skill synthesis using GPT-5.5, task-specific skills improve SpreadsheetBench Pass@1 from 0.397 to 0.505 and BigCodeBench Pass@1 from 0.517 to 0.651. On ALFWorld, the method matches a heavier memory-learning baseline within four points of success rate while producing the shortest successful trajectories among reported methods. Ablations on SpreadsheetBench further show that synthesized skills outperform raw trajectory prompting, that top-$k$ retrieval should stay small, and that failed trajectories are especially useful because they expose recurring evaluator-facing mistakes.

</details>


### [20] [Response-free item difficulty modelling for multiple-choice items with fine-tuned transformers: Component-wise representation and multi-task learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16991)
*Jan Netík,Patrícia Martinková*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Transformer微调的端到端方法，通过联合编码题目文本来建模阅读理解选择题的难度，并探索了组件编码和多任务学习两种扩展，实验表明联合编码是特征工程的有效替代，多任务变体在小样本场景下表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无响应项目难度建模可减少对基于响应的校准的依赖，但阅读理解选择题的难度取决于措辞组件间的推理需求，现有方法依赖手工特征工程和预处理，会丢失信息。

Method: 直接微调Transformer编码器对题目措辞进行端到端联合编码；提出组件变体（通过共享编码器分别编码措辞组件）和多任务变体（保留联合编码并添加辅助多项选择问答目标）。在蒙特卡洛子采样设计下，以三种训练集规模在测试集上评估。

Result: 联合编码是特征工程管道的可行端到端替代方案；组件变体未见显著优势（自注意力已捕获跨组件信号）；多任务变体在小样本场景下带来显著成对改进。

Conclusion: Transformer微调，特别是通过合适的辅助任务正则化，能在应用测量典型训练集规模下恢复大部分措辞可导出的信号；该框架为心理测量学动机的扩展提供了可定制的接口。

Abstract: Response-free item difficulty modelling promises to reduce reliance on response-based calibration but is intrinsically difficult on reading-comprehension multiple-choice items, where difficulty depends on inferential demands across wording components. Whereas most existing approaches extract item-text features and pass them to a separate statistical or machine-learning model, we fine-tune transformer encoders end-to-end on the item wording, eliminating the manual feature engineering and preprocessing that discards information. Moreover, two extensions to this joint-encoding approach are proposed: a component-wise variant that encodes wording components separately through a shared encoder, and a multi-task variant that retains joint encoding and adds an auxiliary multiple-choice question answering objective on the shared encoder. Each method is evaluated under a Monte Carlo subsampling design at three training-set sizes on a held-out test set. We find that joint encoding is a viable end-to-end alternative to feature-engineering pipelines; while the component-wise variant shows no detectable benefit, consistent with self-attention already harvesting the cross-component signal, the multi-task variant delivers significant paired improvements in the smallest-sample regime. Transformer fine-tuning, especially if regularised by a suitable auxiliary task, recovers a substantial share of the wording-derivable signal at training-set sizes typical of applied measurement. The framework provides a customisable interface for psychometrically motivated extensions.

</details>


### [21] [Evaluation Drift in LLM Personality Induction: Are We Moving the Goalpost?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16996)
*Prateek Rajput,Yewei Song,Iyiola E. Olatunji,Jacques Klein,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过对大语言模型进行长篇作文微调以诱导其人格，并使用IPIP-NEO问卷评估稳定性与保真度，发现微调虽能降低回答波动，但五维人格准确率仍接近随机水平，表明无引导的文本缺乏表达忠实人格所需的线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型能否可靠地表达类人的人格特质，还是仅仅模仿表面特征而缺乏稳定的内在人格轮廓。

Method: 使用长篇作文数据对大语言模型进行微调（SFT、DPO、ORPO），每篇作文关联目标大五人格画像，然后通过IPIP-NEO问卷评估诱导人格的稳定性（提示语重述下的得分方差）和保真度（与目标人格的匹配程度）。

Result: 微调一致降低了五个模型在问卷回答中的方差，缓解了预训练模型的评估脆弱性问题；但完整五维人格准确率仍接近随机水平，尽管单特质的得分有所改善。

Conclusion: 无引导的作文缺乏忠实表达人格所需的线索，因此建议构建场景化的数据集或采用交互式方法，通过随时间累积测试对齐的证据来实现人格诱导。

Abstract: Can large language models reliably express a human-like personality, or are they merely mimicking surface cues without a stable underlying profile? To investigate this, we induce personality in LLMs by fine-tuning them on the long-form essays, where each essay is associated with a target Big Five personality profile. We then evaluate the stability and fidelity of the induced personality using the IPIP-NEO questionnaire. Specifically, we ask: (i) does post-training (SFT, DPO, ORPO) stabilize questionnaire scores under prompt rephrasings, and (ii) can it induce target Big Five profiles from unguided essays? Our results demonstrate that fine-tuning consistently reduces variance in questionnaire responses across five models, directly mitigating the evaluation fragility reported in pre-trained models. However, this newfound stability reveals a more fundamental limitation: accuracy on the full five-dimensional profile remains near chance, even when single-trait scores improve. This indicates that unguided essays lack the cues needed for faithful personality expression. We therefore argue for scenario-grounded datasets or interactive elicitation that accumulates test-aligned evidence over time.

</details>


### [22] [HalluScore: Large Language Model Hallucination Question Answering Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17007)
*Aisha Alansari,Hamzah Luqman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HalluScore，一个专门用于评估阿拉伯语大语言模型幻觉问题的结构化问答基准，包含827个精心设计的问题，覆盖不同推理难度、知识领域、历史时间线和阿拉伯文化场景，并对17个模型进行了全面分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉评测基准主要集中于英文和中文，阿拉伯语因标注资源稀缺和词法复杂性而严重缺乏相关基准，无法充分反映阿拉伯语的语言、文化和推理特性。

Method: 通过质量保证、清晰度和事实有效性过滤、模型驱动选择的结构化管线构建827个问题，每个问题配有验证过的地面真相证据、答案解释和多标签标注，并对17个阿拉伯语、多语言和推理LLM进行实证分析。

Result: 阿拉伯语LLM的幻觉不仅涉及事实不准确，还包含文化理解、语言推理和逻辑一致性方面的挑战；提供了所有被评估模型的高质量人工标注（幻觉、非幻觉、部分幻觉）。

Conclusion: HalluScore为阿拉伯语LLM的幻觉评估提供了系统化基准，有助于提升模型在阿拉伯语环境下的可靠性和文化能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in natural language generation, but remain susceptible to hallucination. In response to growing concerns about hallucinations, several benchmarks have been developed, primarily in English and Chinese. However, Arabic remains underrepresented, with limited benchmarks for LLMs hallucination due to scarce annotated resources and the language's morphological complexity. Consequently, existing benchmarks do not adequately reflect the linguistic, cultural, and reasoning characteristics of Arabic. To address this gap, we introduce HalluScore, a structured Arabic question answering benchmark designed to evaluate hallucination behavior in LLMs across different levels of reasoning difficulty, various knowledge domains, historical timelines, and culturally grounded Arabic scenarios. It contains 827 carefully curated questions for evaluating, detecting, and mitigating hallucination in LLMs. The dataset was constructed through a structured pipeline involving quality assurance, filtering for clarity and factual validity, and model-driven selection to retain questions that consistently trigger hallucinations. Each question is linked to verified ground-truth evidence, answer explanations, and multi-label annotations. Using the HalluScore benchmark, we conduct a comprehensive empirical analysis of hallucination patterns across 17 Arabic, multilingual, and reasoning LLMs. Moreover, we provide high-quality human annotations identifying hallucinated, non-hallucinated, and partially hallucinated responses of all evaluated LLMs. These results suggest that hallucination in Arabic LLMs extends beyond factual inaccuracies, encompassing challenges related to cultural understanding, linguistic reasoning, and logical consistency. We release HalluScore to support future research on improving the reliability and cultural competence of LLMs in Arabic.

</details>


### [23] [PARALLAX: Separating Genuine Hallucination Detection from Benchmark Construction Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17028)
*Khizar Hussain,Murat Kantarcioglu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示当前幻觉检测领域的进展很大程度上源于基准数据集中的构造伪影（输入提示直接包含真实答案），在去除这些伪影后，大多数现有方法性能接近随机水平，仅SAPLMA和DRIFT两种监督探针方法表现一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）在医疗、法律和科学等领域的自信幻觉会直接造成危害，从模型内部状态检测幻觉是实现安全部署的关键路径。然而，现有方法在广泛使用的基准上取得了高检测性能，但本文质疑这些进展是否真实有效。

Method: 1) 识别六个语料库中四个存在构造伪影（输入提示直接嵌入真实答案）；2) 提出朴素文本相似度基线TxTemb利用该伪影达到接近完美的检测分数；3) 在去除伪影后进行大规模评估，涵盖22种检测方法、12个开源模型（6个架构家族）和6个语料库；4) 引入DRIFT（基于层间隐藏状态转换的监督探针）作为实时生成检测的对比点。

Result: 1) TxTemb利用构造伪影达到接近完美的检测分数；2) 去除伪影后，大多数现有方法性能接近随机水平；3) 仅SAPLMA和DRIFT（均为上层隐藏状态的监督探针）表现一致。

Conclusion: 幻觉检测领域的报告进展很大程度上被基准构造伪影所解释，在受控条件下大多数基线方法表现不佳，唯有上层隐藏状态的监督探针方法保持有效。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) hallucinate with confidence: their outputs can be fluent, authoritative, and simply wrong. In medical, legal, and scientific applications this failure causes direct harm, and detecting it from internal model states offers a path to safer deployment. A growing body of work reports that this problem is increasingly tractable, with recent methods achieving high detection performance on widely used benchmarks. We show, however, that much of this apparent progress does not survive scrutiny. Four of the six corpora embed the ground-truth answer directly in the input prompt. A naïve text-similarity baseline we call \textsc{TxTemb} exploits this to achieve near-perfect detection scores without any access to model internals. To measure what genuine detection capability remains once these artifacts are controlled, we conduct a large-scale evaluation spanning twenty-two detection methods, twelve open-source models spanning six architectural families, and six corpora. We further introduce \textbf{DRIFT}, a supervised probe over inter-layer hidden-state transitions, as a point of comparison for live-generation detection. Our findings suggest that the field's reported progress on hallucination detection is substantially explained by benchmark construction artifacts in widely used corpora, and that the majority of established baselines perform near chance under controlled conditions; the consistent exceptions are SAPLMA and DRIFT, both supervised probes on upper-layer hidden states.

</details>


### [24] [Agentic AI Translate: An Agentic Translator Prototype for Translation as Communication Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17041)
*Masaru Yamada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic AI Translate——一个基于智能体循环架构的翻译原型系统，将翻译研究元语言转化为生成式AI的指令代码，用四阶段智能体循环（识别→提示→生成→验证）替代传统文本输入/输出范式，强调翻译是沟通设计而非文本转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Yamada（即将发表）的论点付诸实践——即翻译研究的元语言已成为生成式AI的指令代码，挑战机器翻译领域占主导地位的纯文本输入/输出范式，推动翻译从文本转换转向沟通设计。

Method: 系统包含两个阶段：(1) 交互式规范阶段——通过模型辅助对话，基于目的论、语域、受众和体裁惯例构建结构化翻译简报；(2) 四阶段智能体循环——识别、提示、生成、验证。验证阶段采用GEMBA-MQM错误跨度协议进行证据评分，通过DelTA-lite专有名词记忆和运行式双语摘要保持文档级连贯性。

Result: 本文提供了概念性和架构性贡献——一个可执行的翻译即沟通设计理念的实现，描述了哲学动机、架构承诺、四种参考材料类别以及架构暴露的主要设计张力。实证验证留待未来工作。

Conclusion: Agentic AI Translate证明在生成式AI时代，翻译应被理解为沟通设计而非文本转换，其智能体架构为翻译研究元语言与AI指令代码之间的映射提供了具体的可执行方案。

Abstract: We present Agentic AI Translate, an agentic translator prototype that operationalises the thesis of Yamada (forthcoming) -- that the metalanguage of Translation Studies has become an instruction code for generative AI. The system replaces the dominant text-in / text-out paradigm of machine translation with a four-stage agentic cycle (Identify -> Prompt -> Generate -> Verify), preceded by an interactive specification phase in which the user composes -- through model-assisted dialogue -- a structured translation brief grounded in skopos theory, register, audience, and genre conventions. The verification stage adopts the GEMBA-MQM error-span protocol (Kocmi & Federmann, 2023) for evidence-grounded scoring, and document-level coherence is preserved through a DelTA-lite memory of proper nouns and a running bilingual summary, after Wang et al. (2025). We describe the philosophical motivation, the architectural commitments, the four reference-material categories the system consumes, and the principal design tensions the architecture makes explicit. Empirical validation is left for future work; the contribution here is conceptual and architectural -- an executable embodiment of the position that translation in the GenAI era is communication design, not text conversion.

</details>


### [25] [Can LLMs Think Like Consumers? Benchmarking Crowd-Level Reaction Reconstruction with ConsumerSimBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17079)
*Tianyu Wang,Jiajun Li,Jianghao Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ConsumerSimBench基准，包含1,553个真实中文社交媒体话题和23,122条可审计反应标准，将消费者模拟重构为可预测性问题。评估发现最强模型Gemini-3.1-Pro仅覆盖47.8%的真实反应标准，揭示了前沿LLM在技术基准与社会化消费者直觉之间的巨大鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM消费者模拟评估很少检验模型能否重建真实消费者在公共 discourse 中的具体反应模式，缺乏可审计、细粒度的评估方法。

Method: 构建ConsumerSimBench基准，包含1,553个中文社交媒体话题和23,122条原子化、规则审计的反应标准，覆盖四类反应家族。采用逐点式二元判断任务替代整体式偏好评分，将评估分解为可审计的yes-no决策，并设计generate-reflect多智能体pipeline提升性能。

Result: 逐点式判断将三位评审一致率从65.8%提升至92.1%，与人类多数标签一致率达98.4%；最强模型Gemini-3.1-Pro仅覆盖47.8%真实反应标准，GPT-5.2和Claude-4.6表现更差；直接结构化推理提示降低覆盖率，而多智能体pipeline将MiMo-V2.5-Pro从32.9%提升至37.6%。

Conclusion: 前沿LLM在技术基准上的优势无法迁移到高语境中文消费者 discourse 中的社会直觉预测，消费者模拟应重新定义为对真实公共 discourse 反应的预测问题，现有模型距离可靠预测消费者真实关注点仍有显著差距。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used as ``digital consumers'' to simulate public opinion, pre-test marketing decisions, and anticipate audience response. However, existing evaluations rarely ask whether a model can reconstruct the concrete reaction patterns that real consumers surface in public discourse. We introduce ConsumerSimBench, a benchmark built from 1,553 real Chinese social-media topics and 23,122 atomic, rule-audited criteria spanning four reaction families. Rather than scoring open-ended generations with a holistic preference judge, ConsumerSimBench decomposes each task into auditable yes-no decisions over concrete reaction points, raising three-judge agreement from 65.8% to 92.1% with 98.4% agreement between pointwise judge decisions and human-majority labels. Across 13 frontier generators, the strongest model, Gemini-3.1-Pro, covers only 47.8% of real reaction criteria, while GPT-5.2 and Claude-4.6 trail far behind despite their strength on technical benchmarks. The failures reveal a sharp gap between technical-benchmark performance and socially grounded consumer intuition. A direct structured reasoning prompt decreases coverage, while a generate--reflect multi-agent pipeline improves MiMo-V2.5-Pro from 32.9% to 37.6% on a subset. ConsumerSimBench reframes consumer simulation as a forecasting problem over real public-discourse reactions, showing that frontier LLMs remain far from reliably predicting what consumers will actually care about in high-context Chinese consumer discourse.

</details>


### [26] [ACIL: Auto Chain of Thoughts for In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17088)
*Rui Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Auto-CoT框架，通过自动构建包含中间推理步骤的示例来增强上下文学习（ICL），从而提升大语言模型在复杂推理任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ICL在多步推理任务上表现不佳，因为提供的示例通常只有输入-输出对，缺乏显式的中间推理步骤，导致模型难以学习复杂的推理过程。

Method: Auto-CoT框架通过三个步骤增强ICL：1) 为输入-输出示例自动生成推理链；2) 将结构化中间解释扩充到提示上下文中；3) 通过系统化选择过程去除不相关或低质量的示例。

Result: 在多个推理任务上的实验表明，Auto-CoT通过提供显式的中间推理指导，显著提升了ICL的性能。

Conclusion: 将高质量的推理示例纳入ICL提示中，能够引导模型进行更可靠的推理，提高预测准确性。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have shown that Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning can substantially improve performance on complex reasoning tasks. At the same time, In-Context Learning (ICL) has become an important mechanism for adapting LLMs to new tasks without updating model parameters, using only examples provided in the prompt. However, standard ICL often struggles on tasks that require multi-step reasoning, because the demonstrations usually contain only input-output pairs and lack explicit intermediate reasoning steps. This paper introduces an Automatic Chain-of-Thought (Auto-CoT) framework to improve ICL by automatically constructing reasoning-enhanced demonstrations. Auto-CoT generates reasoning chains for input-output examples, augments the prompt context with structured intermediate explanations, and removes irrelevant or low-quality demonstrations through a systematic selection process. By incorporating high-quality reasoning examples into the ICL prompt, Auto-CoT guides the model toward more reliable reasoning and improves prediction accuracy. Experiments across multiple reasoning tasks demonstrate that the proposed framework improves ICL performance by providing explicit intermediate reasoning guidance.

</details>


### [27] [HyDRA: Hybrid Dynamic Routing Architecture for Heterogeneous LLM Pools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17106)
*Aashna Garg,Siddharth Singha Roy,Jinu Jang,Federico Brancasi,Shengyu Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HyDRA 是一个面向生产级 LLM 部署的混合动态路由架构，通过 ModernBERT 编码器与独立 sigmoid 头对查询进行多维度能力需求预测（推理、代码生成、调试、工具使用），并采用短额匹配算法从异构模型池中选择最便宜的满足需求的模型。该框架与模型目录完全解耦，增删模型只需修改配置、无需重新训练。在 SWE-Bench Verified 等基准上，HyDRA 在达到或超越最强模型性能的同时，实现 12.9%-72.5% 的成本节约，并首次在 LLM 路由文献中展示了跨 CJK、欧洲等语系的语言无关路由能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 路由方法在模型池异构化（成本相差数量级）场景下存在两个核心问题：一是仅做二元的强-弱模型二元决策，粒度不足；二是学习参数与特定模型身份耦合，模型目录变更时必须重新训练，无法适应生产环境频繁的模型更新迭代需求。

Method: HyDRA 由两部分组成：(1) 一个 ModernBERT 编码器 + K=4 个独立 sigmoid 头，为每个查询预测推理、代码生成、调试、工具使用四个维度的能力需求分数；(2) 一个短额匹配算法，将预测需求与配置定义的多维模型能力画像进行匹配，选择满足需求的最便宜模型。预测器在 CPU 上运行时延仅 86ms，且完全独立于模型目录。

Result: 在包含 5 个模型（成本跨度数量级）的池上：(1) 峰值质量模式下以 12.9% 成本节约超越最强基线 Claude Sonnet 4.6（75.4% vs 74.2%）；(2) 等质量模式下与 Sonnet 持平且节省 54.1% 成本，较此前内部二元路由器的 9.1% 提升 6 倍；(3) 激进模式以 3.2 个百分点的质量代价实现 72.5% 成本节约。结果在 LiveCodeBench、BigCodeBench、tau-bench 上泛化验证，并首次实现语言无关的跨语系路由。

Conclusion: HyDRA 通过解耦路由学习与模型目录、引入多维度细粒度能力预测和短额匹配，在保持或超越最优模型质量的同时大幅降低成本，并具备零重训的模型目录可扩展性。该系统已在 GitHub Copilot VS Code Chat 自动模式中全面部署。

Abstract: Production LLM deployments increasingly maintain heterogeneous model pools spanning order-of-magnitude cost differences. Existing routers make binary strong-vs-weak decisions and couple learned parameters to specific model identities, requiring retraining whenever the catalog changes. We present HyDRA (Hybrid Dynamic Routing Architecture), a framework that predicts fine-grained, multi-dimensional capability requirements per query and matches them against configuration-defined model profiles via shortfall matching. A ModernBERT encoder with K=4 independent sigmoid heads scores each query along reasoning, code generation, debugging, and tool use; a shortfall-matching algorithm then selects the cheapest model whose capabilities meet the predicted requirements. The deployed predictor runs at 86 ms median CPU inference latency in production, and is fully decoupled from the model catalog -- adding or removing models requires only a configuration change, with zero retraining. On SWE-Bench Verified (5-model pool: GPT-5.4-mini, Claude Haiku 4.5, GPT-5.3 Codex, Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5.4), HyDRA's tunable shortfall threshold spans three regimes: peak-quality exceeds the always-strong Claude Sonnet 4.6 baseline (75.4% vs. 74.2% resolution) at 12.9% cost savings; iso-quality matches Sonnet at 54.1% cost savings, a 6x improvement over our prior in-house binary router at 9.1%; aggressive pushes savings to 72.5% for a 3.2-point quality trade. Results generalize across LiveCodeBench, BigCodeBench, and tau-bench. HyDRA is deployed to all users in GitHub Copilot's VS Code Chat auto-mode and -- to our knowledge for the first time in the LLM routing literature -- demonstrates language-invariant routing across CJK, European, and other script families.

</details>


### [28] [The Point of No Return: Counterfactual Localization of Deceptive Commitment in Language-Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17113)
*Scott Merrill,Shashank Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实定位方法，构建包含5个环境、约146万条句子的大规模语料库，研究语言模型在推理过程中何时产生欺骗性承诺，发现基于注意力的转换特征比词汇线索更具跨环境泛化能力，并识别出可因果抑制欺骗承诺的注意力头子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有欺骗数据集将欺骗视为最终输出的属性，而非推理轨迹的函数，这掩盖了一个更根本的问题：语言模型何时变得致力于欺骗？

Method: 提出反事实定位方法：固定推理轨迹中的每个句子前缀，重新采样后续内容，估计产生欺骗性结果的概率。构建了战略虚张声势、迷宫导航、财务建议、二手车销售和报价谈判五个环境，欺骗从未被提示而是从策略激励中涌现。语料库包含约146万条句子，来自94.1M个采样续写、91.5B个生成token和超过10万个场景。

Result: 句子级人工评估证实检测到的承诺点对应可解释的决策状态转变。词汇线索在跨环境承诺预测中泛化能力差，而基于注意力的转换特征能够跨领域泛化。识别出不到10%的注意力头子集，在一个环境上选择后能因果抑制其他环境中的欺骗承诺。

Conclusion: 欺骗性承诺反映在可复用的推理动态变化而非表面形式中。发布该语料库作为研究语言模型推理中欺骗和更广泛承诺现象的基础资源。

Abstract: Existing deception datasets label completed outputs as honest or deceptive, treating deception as a property of the final response rather than a function of the model's reasoning trace. This obscures a more fundamental question: when does a language model become committed to deception? We introduce counterfactual localization: for each sentence prefix in a reasoning trace, we fix the prefix, resample continuations, and estimate the probability of a deceptive outcome. To scale this, we construct five environments (spanning strategic bluffing, maze guidance, financial advice, used-car sales, and offer negotiation) in which deception is never prompted but emerges from strategic incentives and labels follow mechanically from environment state rather than subjective human judgment. The resulting corpus localizes $\sim$1.46M sentences across four reasoning models, drawn from over 94.1M sampled continuations, 91.5B generated tokens, and over 100K scenarios. Sentence-level human evaluation confirms that detected commitment points correspond to interpretable shifts in decision state. Using this resource, we show that lexical cues for commitment prediction transfer poorly across environments, whereas attention-based transition features generalize out of distribution, suggesting that deceptive commitment is reflected in reusable changes in reasoning dynamics rather than surface form. We further identify compact attention-head sets (under 10% of heads) that, selected on one environment, causally suppress deceptive commitment across held-out environments. We release the corpus as a substrate for studying deception, and more broadly commitment, in language-model reasoning.

</details>


### [29] [Multilingual and Multimodal LLMs in the Wild: Building for Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17152)
*Firoj Alam,Shammur Absar Chowdhury,Enamul Hoque Prince*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该教程概述了多语言多模态大模型（文本、语音、视觉）在低资源、低计算预算下的研究进展，涵盖数据创建、三模态对齐适配器、文化感知评估，并提供动手资源以微调紧凑型多语言VLM和构建语音-文本-LLM流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型从视觉-语言向三模态（看、听、读）演进，但相关流水线和基准测试仍以英语为中心且计算开销大，缺乏面向低资源语言场景的系统性方法和实践指导。

Method: 内容组织为半日交互式教程，综合梳理基础理论、多语言模型（PALO、Maya）、语音-文本LLM进展；提出低成本数据创建与整理策略；设计用于三模态对齐的适配器堆叠方法；构建超越英语的文化感知评估体系；提供紧凑型多语言VLM微调与语音→文本→LLM流水线搭建的实操资源。

Result: 形成一套面向低资源语言环境下多语言多模态AI研究的系统化教程框架，涵盖从理论到实践的完整链路。

Conclusion: 该教程为在低资源语言环境中从事多语言、多模态AI的研究人员和从业者提供了可操作的指导和实践资源，有助于推动该领域的普及与发展。

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs are evolving from vision-language to tri-modality that see, hear, and read, yet pipelines and benchmarks remain English-centric and compute-heavy. The tutorial offers an overview of this emerging research area for multilingual multimodality across text, speech, and vision under limited data/compute budgets, synthesizing foundations, recent multilingual models (PALO, Maya), speech-text LLMs. We cover low-cost data creation/curation; adapter stacks for tri-modal alignment; culture-aware evaluation beyond English and hands on resources for fine-tuning a compact multilingual VLM and wiring a speech->text->LLM pipeline. The content will be delivered as an interactive half-day tutorial, designed for researchers and practitioners working on multilingual, multimodal AI in low-resource language settings.

</details>


### [30] [PluRule: A Benchmark for Moderating Pluralistic Communities on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17187)
*Zoher Kachwala,Bao Tran Truong,Rasika Muralidharan,Haewoon Kwak,Jisun An,Filippo Menczer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了PluRule基准，用于评估AI模型在多模态、多语言、多社区的社交媒体内容审核中的表现。研究发现，即使是GPT-5.2等最先进的视觉-语言模型，在检测社区特定规则违规方面也表现不佳，仅略优于简单基线，表明多元社区的内容审核对语言模型仍是一个根本性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体正朝着多元主义方向发展——由社区自行定义和治理规则。在不同社区中，同一内容可能在一个社区违规，在另一个社区却完全可接受。因此，研究AI模型能否帮助审核这些多元社区的规则违规行为，是本文的核心动机。

Method: 将任务形式化为多项选择问题，模拟人类审核员的工作方式：给定评论及其上下文，判断其违反了哪条特定规则（如有）。构建了PluRule基准，包含来自1,989个Reddit社区的13,371个规则违规样本，涵盖2,885条规则和9种语言，支持多模态（视觉+文本）和多语言场景。

Result: 最先进的视觉-语言模型（如GPT-5.2）表现显著不佳，仅略优于简单基线；更大的模型和更多上下文提供的改进微乎其微；通用性规则（如文明用语、自我推广）更容易被检测到。

Conclusion: 社交媒体中多元社区的内容审核对当前语言模型仍是一个根本性挑战，现有AI在理解和适应不同社区特定规则方面能力有限。

Abstract: Social media are shifting towards pluralism -- community-governed platforms where groups define their own norms. What violates rules in one community may be perfectly acceptable in another. Can AI models help moderate such pluralistic communities? We formalize the task as a multiple-choice problem, mirroring how human moderators operate in the real world: given a comment and its surrounding context, identify which specific rule, if any, is violated. We introduce PluRule, a multimodal, multilingual benchmark for detecting 13,371 rule violations across 1,989 Reddit communities spanning 2,885 rules in 9 languages. Using this benchmark, we show that state-of-the-art vision-language models struggle significantly: even GPT-5.2 with high reasoning performs only slightly better than a trivial baseline. We also find that bigger models and increased context provide marginal gains, and universal rules like civility and self-promotion are easier to detect. Our results show that moderation of pluralistic communities on social media is a fundamental challenge for language models. Our code and benchmark are publicly available.

</details>


### [31] [LLMs for automatic annotation of Mandarin narrative transcripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17205)
*Qingwen Zhao,Hongao Zhu,Yunqi He,Rui Wang,Aijun Huang,Hai Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大型语言模型（LLMs）在中文口语叙事宏观结构标注中的表现，发现最佳模型与人类标注者的一致性（k=.794）接近人类间信度（k=.872），可减少65%的标注时间，但在语义细微区分和词汇多样性高的语料上仍存在挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口语语料的语言标注对语言习得、语言障碍和社会语言学研究至关重要，但传统人工标注耗时费力。尽管LLMs在自动标注任务上展现出潜力，其在非英语语言的复杂语篇层面标注能力仍缺乏系统研究。

Method: 以多语言叙事评估工具（MAIN）为测试平台，对比四种LLMs与经过训练的人工标注员，对儿童、年轻成人和老年人产出的中文口语叙事语料进行叙事宏观结构标注，并评估一致性与效率。

Result: 最佳模型与人工标注的一致性（k=.794）接近人工间信度（k=.872），标注时间减少65%；轻量级本地部署模型表现显著较差。标注难度因宏观结构元素类型而异，需要细微语义区分的类别持续存在挑战；模型在词汇变化大、语义模糊且多元素整合的年轻成人叙事上可靠性下降。

Conclusion: LLMs能有效支持非英语口语语料的语篇层面标注，但在语义复杂的任务中仍需人工监督。研究提供了开源提示模板供后续使用。

Abstract: Linguistic annotation of transcribed speech is essential for research in language acquisition, language disorders, and sociolinguistics, yet remains labor-intensive and time-consuming. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise in automating annotation tasks, their ability to handle complex discourse-level annotation in non-English languages remains understudied. This study evaluates whether LLMs can reliably annotate narrative macrostructure-the hierarchical organization of story grammar elements-in spoken Mandarin, using the Multilingual Assessment Instrument for Narratives (MAIN) as a testbed. We compared four LLMs against trained human annotators on narratives produced by children, young adults, and older adults. The best-performing model achieved agreement with human raters (k=.794) approaching human-human reliability levels (k=.872) while reducing annotation time by 65%, whereas the locally deployable lightweight model performed substantially worse. Annotation difficulty varied systematically by macrostructure element type, with categories requiring subtle semantic differentiation posing persistent challenges. Furthermore, model reliability decreased on young adult narratives, which exhibited greater lexical variation, semantic ambiguity, and multi-element integration within single utterances. These findings suggest that LLMs can effectively support discourse-level annotation in non-English spoken corpora, while highlighting the continued need for human oversight in semantically complex tasks. Our prompt templates are open sourced for future use.

</details>


### [32] [Artificial Intolerance: Stigmatizing Language in Clinical Documentation Skews Large Language Model Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17228)
*Jen-tse Huang,Didi Zhou,Faith Kamau,Amy Oh,Anne R. Links,Mark Dredze,Mary Catherine Beach,Somnath Saha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了前沿LLMs在处理临床文本时对污名化语言（SL）的偏见传播问题。实验覆盖9个模型、4种污名化疾病，通过注入不同强度和类型的SL（怀疑、责备、诽谤），发现所有模型均表现出显著偏见，导致临床决策偏向保守。即使单句SL也足以改变模型输出，呈现明显的剂量-反应关系。标准缓解策略（CoT推理、自我去偏）效果有限，模型难以显式识别SL但仍受其隐性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs正被部署于临床决策支持等高风险领域，但模型对临床笔记中常见污名化语言的鲁棒性尚未被充分探索，这种偏见可能导致健康差异的自动化与固化。

Method: 选取9个前沿LLMs，针对4种被污名化的疾病，在临床案例中注入不同强度和类型的SL（怀疑、责备、诽谤），评估模型对患者管理决策的偏差；同时测试CoT推理和自我去偏等基于提示的缓解策略。

Result: 所有模型均表现出显著偏见，临床决策向更保守的患者管理倾斜；单句SL即能改变模型输出，呈现剂量-反应关系；CoT和自我去偏缓解策略效果有限，模型无法显式识别SL但仍受其隐性影响。

Conclusion: 当前LLMs在临床NLP中的公平性和鲁棒性存在严重漏洞，亟需严格的算法防护措施以防止健康差异的自动化。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes domains such as clinical decision support and medical documentation. However, the robustness of these models against subtle linguistic variations, specifically stigmatizing language (SL) commonly found in human-authored clinical notes, remains critically under-explored. In this work, we investigate whether frontier LLMs inherit and propagate this human bias when processing clinical text. We systematically evaluate nine frontier LLMs across four stigmatized medical conditions, utilizing clinical vignettes injected with varying intensities and phenotypes of SL (doubt, blame, and maligning). Our results demonstrate that all evaluated models exhibit substantial bias, with clinical decision-making significantly skewed towards less aggressive patient management. Notably, we observe a high sensitivity to linguistic framing, where a single SL sentence is sufficient to alter model outputs, revealing a clear dose-response relationship. Furthermore, we evaluate standard prompt-based mitigation strategies, including Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning and model self-debiasing. These approaches show limited efficacy; models struggle to explicitly identify SL while remaining implicitly influenced by it. Our findings expose a critical vulnerability in current LLMs regarding fairness and robustness in clinical NLP, underscoring the need for rigorous algorithmic guardrails to prevent the automation of health disparities.

</details>


### [33] [OProver: A Unified Framework for Agentic Formal Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17283)
*David Ma,Kaijing Ma,Shawn Guo,Yunfeng Shi,Enduo Zhao,Jiajun Shi,Zhaoxiang Zhang,Gavin Cheung,Jiaheng Liu,Zili Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OProver是一个统一的Lean 4形式化定理证明智能体框架，通过迭代式后训练（结合检索增强的证明修复、SFT和RL）以及大规模证明库OProofs（含1.77M语句和6.86M已验证证明），在多个基准测试上达到领先水平（MiniF2F Pass@32达93.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形式化定理证明训练主要依赖大规模证明生成和验证器感知训练，但智能体式证明（agentic proving）仅在推理时使用，未被集成到证明器训练过程中。作者希望将智能体式证明纳入训练循环，使模型能从失败尝试中迭代学习。

Method: OProver采用统一框架，在Lean 4中通过检索已验证证明和编译器反馈迭代修正失败证明。训练流程包括：持续预训练 + 迭代后训练（每轮执行智能体式证明，将新验证的证明索引到OProofs和检索记忆，用修复轨迹做SFT，用未解决难题做RL）。OProofs由公共Lean资源、大规模证明合成和智能体证明轨迹构建而成。

Result: OProver-32B在MiniF2F上Pass@32达93.3%，ProverBench 58.2%，PutnamBench 11.3%，均取得最优；在MathOlympiad（22.8%）和ProofNet（33.2%）上排名第二，整体上优于所有现有开源全证明证明器。

Conclusion: 将智能体式证明集成到训练循环中，通过检索增强的迭代修复和强化学习，能显著提升形式化定理证明模型的性能，OProver代表了当前开源全证明证明器的最强水平。

Abstract: Recent progress in formal theorem proving has benefited from large-scale proof generation and verifier-aware training, but agentic proving is rarely integrated into prover training, appearing only at inference time. We present OProver, a unified framework for agentic formal theorem proving in Lean 4, in which failed proof attempts are iteratively revised using retrieved compiler verified proofs and Lean compiler feedback. OProver is trained through continued pretraining followed by iterative post-training: each iteration runs agentic proving, indexes newly verified proofs into OProofs and the retrieval memory, uses repair trajectories as SFT data, and uses unresolved hard cases for RL. OProofs is built from public Lean resources, large-scale proof synthesis, and agentic proving traces, containing 1.77M Lean statements, 6.86M compiler-verified proofs, and serialized trajectories with retrieved context, failed attempts, feedback, and repairs. Across five benchmarks, OProver-32B attains the best Pass@32 on MiniF2F (93.3%), ProverBench (58.2%), and PutnamBench (11.3%), and ranks second on MathOlympiad (22.8%) and ProofNet (33.2%) more top placements than any prior open-weight whole-proof prover.

</details>


### [34] [ConflictRAG: Detecting and Resolving Knowledge Conflicts in Retrieval Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17301)
*Chenyu Wang,Yingmin Liu,Yang Shu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ConflictRAG是一个冲突感知的RAG框架，通过两阶段冲突检测（嵌入MLP+选择性LLM精炼）、Entropy-TOPSIS信源评估和CARS评估指标，在多个基准测试中实现88.7%冲突检测F1值和5.3-6.1%的正确率提升，同时降低62%的API成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统隐式假设检索文档间相互一致，但实际场景中知识冲突普遍存在，传统方法缺乏系统的冲突检测与消解机制。

Method: 提出三模块框架：(1) 两阶段冲突检测：轻量级嵌入MLP分类器+选择性LLM精炼；(2) Entropy-TOPSIS数据驱动的信源可信度评估；(3) CARS诊断评估指标。

Result: 检测F1达88.7%，正确率较最强基线提升5.3-6.1%，API成本降低62%，信源选择准确率提升7.1%，且跨骨干LLM迁移有效。

Conclusion: ConflictRAG有效解决了RAG系统中的知识冲突问题，在性能、成本和可迁移性方面均优于现有方法，为RAG系统的冲突处理提供了系统化的解决方案和诊断工具。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems implicitly assume mutual consistency among retrieved documents -- an assumption that frequently fails in practice. We present ConflictRAG, a conflict-aware RAG framework that detects, classifies, and resolves knowledge conflicts prior to answer generation. The framework introduces three contributions: (1) a two-stage conflict detection module combining a lightweight embedding-based MLP classifier with selective LLM refinement, reducing API costs by 62% while maintaining 90.8% detection accuracy; (2) an Entropy-TOPSIS framework for data-driven source credibility assessment, improving selection accuracy by 7.1% over manual heuristics; and (3) a Conflict-Aware RAG Score (CARS) for diagnostic evaluation of conflict-handling capabilities. Experiments on three benchmarks against six baselines demonstrate 88.7% conflict-detection F1 and consistent 5.3--6.1% correctness gains over the strongest conflict-aware baseline, with the pipeline transferring effectively across backbone LLMs.

</details>


### [35] [Weak-to-Strong Elicitation via Mismatched Wrong Drafts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17314)
*Wei Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过在较弱学习者的GRPO上下文中注入来自更小但领域训练模型的数学错误草稿（且与当前问题不匹配），显著提升了强学习者的数学推理能力。不匹配的错误草稿变体在MATH-500上达到71.98%，是Mathstral-7B-v0.1模型的最高公布结果，在AIME 2025/2026上也全面领先基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否通过离策略的、来自更小更弱模型的经验，激发强学习者在on-policy RL微调（如GRPO）无法达到的能力。

Method: Dr. GRPO：将来自更小但领域训练模型（Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B）的数学错误草稿（与当前问题不匹配）注入到强学习者（Mathstral-7B）的GRPO上下文中进行训练。

Result: 不匹配变体在MATH-500上达71.98%（Mathstral-7B最高），在AIME 2025/2026上pass@k全面领先基线；不匹配相比匹配错误变体提升+1.62pp（p=0.0015）。

Conclusion: 草稿与问题的不匹配是关键有效成分；该方法在单GPU上无需SFT、奖励模型、合成数据或批评-修正内循环，即取得SOTA结果。

Abstract: We consider whether off-policy experience from a smaller, weaker model can elicit capability in a stronger learner that on-policy RL fine-tuning (e.g., GRPO) does not reach. We find that injecting mathematically wrong drafts from a smaller but more domain-trained model -- mismatched to the current problem -- into a stronger learner's GRPO context consistently outperforms standard on-policy GRPO on held-out MATH-500 and out-of-distribution AIME 2025/2026. Concretely, we use Mathstral-7B as the learner, Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B as the draft model, 8.8K Level 3--5 MATH problems (with MATH-500 held out), and train with Dr. GRPO. Mismatch is an active ingredient: shuffling drafts to mismatched problems while holding everything else constant yields $+1.62$pp on MATH-500 (greedy pass@1) over the matched-wrong variant ($n=10$ seeds, $p=0.0015$, Welch's $t$). In fact, the mismatched-wrong variant leads all other variants we tested on MATH-500 across both greedy pass@1 and sampling pass@$k$. On out-of-distribution AIME 2025 and 2026, the mismatched-wrong variant uniquely lifts pass@$k$ above both Mathstral-7B (in its native [INST] format) and the Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B draft model at every sample budget from $k=1$ to $k=1024$ across 2 seeds ($+14.2$pp on 2025 and $+9.0$pp on 2026 at pass@1024 over Mathstral-7B), and at pass@1024 also leads no-draft, matched-wrong, and mismatched-correct variants on both years. All variants use the same prompt with no draft injection at test time. The recipe -- trained on a single GPU with no SFT, no reward models, no synthesized data, and no produce-critique-revise inner loop -- reaches 71.98% MATH-500 on Mathstral-7B-v0.1, the highest published result on this model to our knowledge, surpassing the heavier WizardMath pipeline at 70.9% on full MATH (SFT + PPO with process/instruction reward models).

</details>


### [36] [Taming "Zombie'' Agents: A Markov State-Aware Framework for Resilient Multi-Agent Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17348)
*Taolin Zhang,Pukun Zhao,Qizhou Chen,Jiuheng Wan,Chen Chen,Xiaofeng He,Chengyu Wang,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AgentRevive框架，通过"活跃/待命/终止"三种状态的软状态转移机制，替代传统硬剪枝方法，实现多智能体系统的弹性演化，在推理和领域任务上优于基线方法并降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体系统采用激进的图演化策略（如节点或边剪枝），由于幻觉或临时知识缺口等问题过早丢弃有价值的智能体，忽略了"僵尸"智能体在后续讨论轮次中恢复并贡献的潜力。

Method: 提出AgentRevive框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 状态感知策略学习：将智能体状态分为Active、Standby、Terminated三种，通过风险评估器优化状态转移，最小化不可靠节点影响的同时保护有价值节点；(2) 状态感知边优化：根据策略学习的状态对子图边进行剪枝，永久移除Terminated节点，保留Standby节点以供后续轮次评估其潜在贡献。

Result: 在通用推理、领域特定任务和幻觉挑战任务上的广泛实验表明，该方法持续优于强基线方法，并通过状态感知智能体调度显著降低token消耗。

Conclusion: 通过软状态转移机制替代硬剪枝，能够保留潜在可恢复的智能体（Standby状态），在保证系统鲁棒性的同时提升效率和性能。

Abstract: Recent advancements in LLM-based multi-agent systems have demonstrated remarkable collaborative capabilities across complex tasks. To improve overall efficiency, existing methods often rely on aggressive graph evolution among agents (e.g., node or edge pruning), which risks prematurely discarding valuable agents due to transient issues such as hallucinations or temporary knowledge gaps. However, such hard pruning overlooks the potential for ``zombie'' agents to recover and contribute in subsequent discussion rounds. In this paper, we propose AgentRevive, a Markov state-aware framework for resilient multi-agent evolution. Our approach dynamically manages agent collaboration through soft state transitions, implemented via two key components: (1) State-Aware Policy Learning: Agent states are divided into ``Active'', ``Standby'', and ``Terminated'' states, selectively propagating messages based on agent memory. The policy employs a risk estimator to optimize agent state transitions by assessing hallucination risk, minimizing the influence of unreliable nodes while safeguarding valuable ones. (2) State-Aware Edge Optimization: Subgraph edges are pruned according to states learned from the policy, permanently removing ``Terminated'' nodes and retaining ``Standby'' nodes for subsequent rounds to assess their potential future contributions. Extensive experiments on general reasoning, domain-specific, and hallucination challenge tasks show that our method consistently outperforms strong baselines and significantly reduces token consumption through state-aware agent scheduling.

</details>


### [37] [AMATA: Adaptive Multi-Agent Trajectory Alignment for Knowledge-Intensive Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17352)
*Taolin Zhang,Dongyang Li,Chen Chen,Qizhou Chen,Jiuheng Wan,Xiaofeng He,Chengyu Wang,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AMATA是一个自适应多智能体轨迹对齐框架，通过六个专业智能体协作和轨迹偏好学习，在知识密集型问答中有效减少幻觉并提升事实一致性，在五个基准上超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在知识密集型问答中仍存在幻觉和长尾知识缺口问题，导致事实一致性不足。

Method: 提出AMATA框架，包含六个专业智能体协作进行复杂问题推理，将多智能体协作形式化为轨迹偏好对齐问题，引入(1)轨迹内偏好学习以优先关键智能体，(2)智能体间依赖学习以通过依赖感知的直接偏好优化技术捕获跨智能体工具依赖。

Result: 在五个知识密集型QA基准上，AMATA一致优于基线方法、知识增强框架和基于LLM的轨迹系统，同时有效降低了token消耗。

Conclusion: AMATA通过多智能体协作与轨迹偏好对齐，有效提升了知识密集型问答的事实一致性和可解释性，同时具有更高的效率。

Abstract: Despite substantial advances in large language models (LLMs), generating factually consistent responses for knowledge-intensive question answering remains challenging. These difficulties are primarily due to hallucinations and the limitations of LLMs in bridging long-tail knowledge gaps. To address this, we propose AMATA, an Adaptive Multi-Agent Trajectory Alignment framework that dynamically integrates external knowledge to improve response interpretability and factual grounding. Our architecture leverages six specialized agents that collaboratively perform structured actions for complex question reasoning. We formalize multi-agent collaboration with external tools as a trajectory preference alignment problem, incorporating question-aware agent customization and inter-agent preference harmonization. AMATA introduces two principal innovations: (1) Intra-Trajectory Preference Learning, which learns objective-oriented preferences to prioritize critical agents, and (2) Inter-Agent Dependency Learning, which captures cross-agent tool dependencies through a novel dependency-aware direct preference optimization technique. Empirical results show that AMATA consistently outperforms baseline approaches, knowledge-augmented frameworks, and LLM-based trajectory systems on five established knowledge-intensive QA benchmarks. Further analysis demonstrates the efficiency of our method in reducing token consumption.

</details>


### [38] [Learning Transferable Topology Priors for Multi-Agent LLM Collaboration Across Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17359)
*Taolin Zhang,Zijie Zhou,Jiuheng Wan,Tingyuan Hu,Chengyu Wang,Xiaofeng He,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TopoPrior框架，通过离线学习可迁移的拓扑先验知识，为多智能体LLM协作生成查询条件化的初始协作图，从而减少在线拓扑搜索开销和推理时token消耗，在跨领域推理基准上提升了异构拓扑演化基线的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于拓扑演化的多智能体协作方法为每个查询从头构建或优化协作拓扑，导致在线搜索开销大、推理时token消耗高，且在跨领域场景下可扩展性有限。

Method: TopoPrior包含两个核心组件：(1) 可迁移拓扑先验学习模块，采用条件变分图框架在隐空间中捕捉跨领域的可复用结构规律；(2) 查询条件化隐空间适配模块，引入对抗对齐以减少不必要的领域差异，同时保留查询相关的结构变化。

Result: 在多领域推理基准上，TopoPrior一致提升了多种异构拓扑演化基线，同时减少了在线推理时的token使用量，且仅增加少量可训练参数。

Conclusion: 可迁移的拓扑初始化是一种有效且轻量级的机制，能够提高跨领域多智能体LLM协作的效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems have shown strong potential for complex reasoning by coordinating specialized agents through structured communication. However, existing topology-evolution methods typically construct or optimize a collaboration topology for each query from scratch, leading to substantial online search overhead, high inference-time token consumption, and limited scalability in multi-domain settings. We propose TopoPrior, a framework for learning transferable topology priors for multi-agent LLM collaboration across domains. Rather than repeatedly searching for effective collaboration structures online, TopoPrior learns reusable topology priors from reference collaboration graphs collected offline from multiple domains and uses them to generate query-conditioned initial collaboration graphs for downstream refinement. By shifting part of topology search from per-query online optimization to offline prior learning, TopoPrior amortizes search cost while remaining compatible with existing topology-evolution backbones. Technically, TopoPrior contains two key components. First, a transferable topology prior learning module employs a conditional variational graph framework to capture reusable structural regularities across domains in a latent space. Second, a query-conditioned latent adaptation module introduces adversarial alignment to reduce unnecessary domain discrepancy while preserving query-relevant structural variation. Experiments on multi-domain reasoning benchmarks show that TopoPrior consistently improves several heterogeneous topology-evolution backbones while reducing online inference-time token usage, with only modest additional trainable parameters. These results suggest that transferable topology initialization is an effective and lightweight mechanism for improving the efficiency of multi-agent LLM collaboration across domains.

</details>


### [39] [NewsLens: A Multi-Agent Framework for Adversarial News Bias Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17364)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NewsLens是一个五智能体对抗式流水线，用于结构化新闻偏见分析，通过事实验证者、渐进框架分析者、保守框架分析者、 propaganda 检测者和中立摘要者协作，将文章解构为可解释的框架图，揭示意识形态遗漏、修辞操纵和框架边界。在15篇文章上评估，发现中心媒体视角分歧得分最高，保守框架媒体操纵指数最高，跨模型验证显示高一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有媒体偏见检测通常被框定为分类任务（为文章或媒体分配政治标签），但这种框架过于浅层——它识别了偏见的存在，但未能揭示偏见在哪里、如何表现，以及关键的结构性遗漏是什么。

Method: 提出NewsLens五智能体对抗式流水线，包含事实验证者、渐进框架分析者、保守框架分析者、 propaganda 检测者和中立摘要者，协作将文章解构为可解释的框架图。评估使用Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct（4位量化，Google Colab T4），并在克什米尔集群上用Mistral 7B进行跨模型验证。使用视角分歧度（PDS）和操纵指数（MI）作为度量指标。

Result: 中心媒体视角分歧度最高（Qwen 0.907, Mistral 0.729）；保守框架媒体操纵指数最高（0.600）；高 propaganda 内容跨模型一致性高（Republic World delta-PDS=0.125, MI=0.8）；Mann-Whitney U检验未发现统计显著差异；部分消融实验显示移除propaganda检测器导致中立摘要者输出遗漏精度下降。

Conclusion: NewsLens将先前的词汇-几何偏见工作扩展为智能体LLM推理，完全可复现，使用开放权重模型且无需API密钥。该方法以结构化方式暴露了新闻中的意识形态遗漏和修辞操纵，超越了简单的分类范式。

Abstract: Media bias detection has predominantly been framed as a classification task: assign a political label to an article or outlet. We argue this framing is too shallow: it identifies that bias exists but not where, how, or crucially, what is structurally omitted. We present NewsLens, a five-agent adversarial pipeline for structured news bias navigation. A Fact Verifier, Progressive Framing Analyst, Conservative Framing Analyst, Propaganda Detector, and Neutral Summarizer collaborate to deconstruct articles into interpretable framing maps, exposing ideological omissions, rhetorical manipulation, and framing boundaries. The system is evaluated on 15 articles across four geopolitical event clusters (India-Pakistan Kashmir, Gaza, Climate Policy, Ukraine) using Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct (4-bit quantised, Google Colab T4), with cross-model validation using Mistral 7B on the Kashmir cluster. Center outlets show the highest mean Perspective Divergence Score (PDS: Qwen 0.907, Mistral 0.729 on Kashmir subset); conservative-framing outlets show the highest mean Manipulation Index (MI: 0.600 across both models). Cross-model comparison shows high consistency for high-propaganda content (Republic World delta-PDS=0.125, MI=0.8 both models) and greater variance for nuanced reporting. Mann-Whitney U tests find no statistically significant between-group differences at n=15, reported honestly as a sample-size limitation confirmed by post-hoc power analysis. A partial ablation removing the Propaganda Detector shows degraded omission precision in the Neutral Summarizer output. The architecture extends prior lexical-geometric bias work to agentic LLM reasoning, and is fully reproducible using open-weight models without API keys.

</details>


### [40] [Learning Faster with Better Tokens: Parameter-Efficient Vocabulary Adaptation for Specialized Text Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17379)
*Gunjan Balde,Soumyadeep Roy,Mainack Mondal,Niloy Ganguly*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合词汇适配与参数高效预训练的领域自适应方法，通过替换未充分训练和不可达的token、扩充领域特定token来解决大语言模型在专业领域（法律、医疗）的词汇不匹配问题，在Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B上验证了该方法在摘要质量、训练效率（降低35-55%训练时间）和参数量（缩减37%）方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用域预训练的语言模型在应用于专业领域时存在分词效率低下的问题，持续的领域预训练虽能部分缓解性能下降，但无法从根本上解决词汇不匹配问题。

Method: 提出一种结合词汇适配与参数高效预训练的统一框架：在预训练分词器中增加领域特定token，同时选择性替换未充分训练和不可达的token以限制参数量增长。在法律和医学摘要任务上，基于挑战性评估协议（聚焦专家驱动的文本和摘要，其中包含高浓度的过度分词的OOV词汇）进行验证。

Result: 词汇适配算法提升了摘要模型的整体质量，增强了生成摘要与参考摘要之间的语义相似度；生成的摘要包含更合适的领域新词，提升了连贯性、相关性和忠实度；训练时间相比持续预训练减少35-55%，参数量相比仅扩充方法减少最多37%。

Conclusion: 提出的词汇替换再扩充方法能有效解决专业领域的词汇不匹配问题，在提升摘要质量的同时显著降低了训练成本和参数规模，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models pretrained on general-domain corpora often exhibit tokenization inefficiencies when applied to specialized domains. Although continual pretraining for domain adaptation partially alleviate performance degradation, it does not resolve the fundamental vocabulary mismatch. To address this gap, we introduce a targeted parameter-efficient domain adaptation approach that combines vocabulary adaptation with pretraining for LLM-based text summarization. Our unified framework augments pretrained tokenizers with domain-specific tokens while selectively replacing under-trained and unreachable tokens to limit parameter growth. We evaluate our approach on Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B across legal and medical summarization tasks on a challenge-oriented evaluation protocol focused on expert-driven text and summaries which typically has higher concentration of over-fragmented Out-of-Vocabulary (OOV) words. The vocabulary adaptation algorithm enhances the overall quality of the summarization model by improving semantic similarity between the generated summaries and their references. In addition, the adapted model produces summaries that incorporate more appropriate novel and domain-specific words, leading to improved coherence, relevance, and faithfulness. We further observe that our proposed approach significantly reduce training time by $35-55\%$ over continual pretraining and reduce parameter counts up to $37\%$ w.r.t expansion-only methods. We make the codebase publicly available at https://github.com/gb-kgp/VocabReplace-Then-Expand.

</details>

Abstract: This paper presents MiniGPT, a compact from-scratch implementation of GPT-style autoregressive language modeling in PyTorch. The aim is to rebuild the core GPT pipeline from first principles after studying the design of nanoGPT by Andrej Karpathy, while keeping the model and training code independently written in a single notebook. MiniGPT implements token and positional embeddings, causal multi-head self-attention, pre-LayerNorm Transformer blocks, residual connections, feed-forward MLP layers, next-token cross-entropy training (teacher forcing), validation tracking, checkpoint selection, and autoregressive text generation. This paper evaluates the implementation on Tiny Shakespeare dataset using character-level tokenization. A baseline 0.83M-parameter model reaches a validation loss of 1.7236 after 3000 training iterations. A stronger 10.77M-parameter configuration, using a larger context length and improved training settings, reaches a best validation loss of 1.4780 and generates text with recognizable Shakespeare-style dialogue structure. MiniGPT does not introduce a new language-model architecture. Instead, it documents a clear and reproducible implementation path from raw text to trained character-level generation, including design choices, training behavior, generation quality, and practical limitations.

</details>


### [42] [BELIEF: Structured Evidence Modeling and Uncertainty-Aware Fusion for Biomedical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17435)
*Chang Zong,Hao Ning,Siliang Tang,Jie Huang,Jian Wan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BELIEF框架，将检索到的文献转换为结构化证据对象，通过符号路径（基于Dempster-Shafer理论的可靠性加权融合）和神经路径（LLM语义推理）两条互补推理路径，以及可靠性感知仲裁模块，实现闭集生物医学问答中结构化的证据建模与不确定性感知融合。在PubMedQA、MedQA和MedMCQA上使用五种通用LLM骨干，BELIEF在30个骨干-数据集-指标设置中取得25个最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强LLM方法将检索文献作为平面文本输入，忽略了证据可靠性差异和不确定性信息，导致决策质量受限。本文旨在通过结构化证据建模和不确定性感知融合提升生物医学问答的可靠性和可解释性。

Method: BELIEF将检索文档转换为包含临床属性、来源质量、问题相关性、支持强度和候选假设的结构化证据对象。符号路径基于D-S理论构建可靠性加权基本概率分配并进行不确定性感知融合；神经路径对相同结构化证据进行LLM语义推理；可靠性感知仲裁模块根据信念强度、不确定性、证据可靠性和语义一致性协调两条路径的输出。

Result: 在PubMedQA、MedQA和MedMCQA三个数据集上使用五种通用LLM骨干，BELIEF在30个设置中取得25个最佳结果。消融、互补性、不确定性分层和成本分析表明BELIEF通过显式化证据结构、路径分歧和决策不确定性，提升了检索证据的利用效果。

Conclusion: BELIEF通过结构化证据建模和双路径不确定性感知融合，显著提升了通用LLM在生物医学问答上的性能。在与生物医学领域专用模型的比较中，BELIEF在MedQA和MedMCQA上具有竞争力，而专用预训练在PubMedQA上仍具优势。

Abstract: Biomedical question answering often requires decisions from retrieved literature whose relevance, quality, and support for candidate answers are uneven. Most retrieval-augmented large language model (LLM) methods feed this literature to the model as flat text, leaving evidence reliability and remaining uncertainty largely implicit. We propose BELIEF, a structured evidence modeling and uncertainty-aware fusion framework for closed-set biomedical question answering. Rather than treating retrieved documents as undifferentiated context, BELIEF converts them into evidence objects that record clinical attributes, source quality, question relevance, support strength, and the associated candidate hypothesis. These evidence objects provide a shared basis for two complementary reasoning paths. The symbolic path constructs reliability-weighted basic probability assignments based on Dempster--Shafer (D-S) theory over a finite answer space and performs uncertainty-aware symbolic evidence fusion to estimate belief and residual uncertainty. The neural path uses the same structured evidence for LLM-based semantic inference, while a reliability-aware arbitration module reconciles the symbolic and neural outputs according to belief strength, uncertainty, evidence reliability, and semantic consistency. Experiments on PubMedQA, MedQA, and MedMCQA with five general-purpose LLM backbones show that BELIEF obtains the best result in 25 of 30 backbone--dataset--metric settings. Comparisons with biomedical-domain models indicate that BELIEF is competitive on MedQA and MedMCQA, while specialized biomedical pretraining remains advantageous on PubMedQA. Ablation, complementarity, uncertainty-stratified, and cost analyses further show that BELIEF improves retrieved-evidence utilization by making evidence structure, path disagreement, and decision uncertainty explicit.

</details>


### [43] [Beyond Catalogue Counts: the Dataset Visibility Asymmetry in Low-Resource Multilingual NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17442)
*Zhiyin Tan,Changxu Duan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过引入资源密度指数（RDI），对比目录记录与研究文献中的数据集可见性，发现许多语言在目录中看似数据贫乏，但在学术文献中却有活跃的数据集活动，揭示了多语言数据稀缺不仅是生产问题，更是文档化、可发现性和长期可访问性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集中式目录（如LRE Map和LDC）仅记录了已注册或机构分发的数据集，无法反映研究文献中实际创建、引用或复用的数据集情况，存在可见性差距。

Method: 1) 基于目录建立基线（LRE Map和LDC），计算200种最广泛使用语言的资源密度指数（RDI = 每百万说话者对应的目录数据集数量）。2) 对141种低可见性语言（RDI < 0.1或为0），使用基于LLM的引文挖掘流程从Semantic Scholar语料库中提取数据集引用，经人工验证和整合后识别数据集及其可访问性。

Result: 118种语言（59%）在目录中的平均RDI为0，另有23种语言RDI低于0.1。通过引文挖掘，在53种低可见性语言中识别出609个独特数据集，其中356个通过有效的公共链接保持开放可访问。

Conclusion: 多语言数据稀缺不仅是生产不足的问题，也是文档化、可发现性和长期可访问性的问题。许多大说话者人口的语言在目录记录中看似数据贫乏，但研究文献中显示了活跃的数据集活动，存在显著的可见性差距。

Abstract: Multilingual NLP often relies on dataset counts from centralized catalogues to characterize which languages are resource-rich or resource-poor. However, these catalogues record only one layer of dataset visibility: what has been registered or institutionally distributed. They do not necessarily reflect which datasets are created, cited, or reused in the research literature. To examine this gap, we combine a catalogue-based baseline with literature-backed evidence of dataset circulation. We introduce the Resource Density Index (RDI), defined as the number of catalogued datasets per one million speakers, and compute it for the 200 most widely spoken languages in Ethnologue. Among them, 118 languages (59%) have an average RDI of zero across the LRE Map and the Linguistic Data Consortium (LDC), and another 23 fall below 0.1, corresponding to at most one catalogued dataset per ten million speakers. We then apply an LLM-assisted citation-mining pipeline over the Semantic Scholar corpus to these 141 low-visibility languages. After manual validation and consolidation, we identify 609 unique datasets across 53 languages, of which 356 remain openly accessible through working public links. These results reveal a substantial visibility gap: many large-speaker languages appear data-poor in catalogue records yet show clear evidence of dataset activity in the research literature. Our findings suggest that multilingual data scarcity should be understood not only as a production problem, but also as a question of documentation, discoverability, and long-term accessibility. Code and data are publicly available at (https://github.com/zhiyintan/dataset-visibility-asymmetry).

</details>


### [44] [VerifyMAS: Hypothesis Verification for Failure Attribution in LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17467)
*Hezhe Qiao,Hanghang Tong,Ee-Peng Lim,Bing Liu,Guansong Pang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出VerifyMAS，一种基于假设验证的框架，通过将故障归因分解为轨迹级错误验证和细粒度智能体定位，解决LLM多智能体系统中全局故障（如跨步不一致和智能体间协调错误）难以自动归因的问题，有效降低搜索空间并提升归因性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-MAS故障归因方法依赖智能体的局部日志，无法捕获仅在整个交互轨迹中显现的全局故障（如跨步不一致和智能体间协调错误）；直接预测智能体-错误对的方式导致巨大的组合搜索空间，难以实现细粒度归因。

Method: 提出VerifyMAS假设验证框架：(1) 将归因分解为轨迹级错误验证和细粒度智能体定位两个阶段，采用"错误优先"的归因方式；(2) 基于结构化错误分类法构建假设驱动的数据构造策略；(3) 微调专门的LLM验证器模型用于轨迹级故障验证和智能体归因。

Result: 在Aegis-Bench和Who&When数据集上，VerifyMAS持续提升了多种骨干模型（包括开源Qwen和基于API的GPT模型）的性能，优于先前方法，且不牺牲长多智能体轨迹的推理效率。

Conclusion: 基于假设验证的归因范式（验证后再定位）能够有效捕获全局故障模式，显著缩小搜索空间，是一种优于直接预测和智能体优先方法的故障归因方案。

Abstract: Large language model-driven multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) excel at complex tasks, yet unreliable agents remain a key bottleneck to system-level reliability. Automatic failure attribution is therefore critical, but existing approaches, such as direct prediction of agent-error pairs and agent-first failure attribution, rely on local logs of agents and miss global failures that only manifest over full interaction trajectories, such as cross-step inconsistencies and inter-agent coordination errors. Moreover, directly predicting failures induces a large combinatorial search space, hindering fine-grained attribution. To address these challenges, we propose VerifyMAS, a hypothesis verification framework for agent failure attribution. Instead of directly predicting faulty agents and error types, VerifyMAS formulates and verifies failure hypotheses against full trajectories. This verification-based approach decomposes attribution into trajectory-level error validation and fine-grained agent localization, providing an error-first attribution approach that captures global failure patterns while substantially reducing the search space. We further introduce a hypothesis-based data construction strategy grounded in a structured error taxonomy and fine-tune a specialized LLM verifier model for trajectory-level failure verification and agent attribution. Experiments on Aegis-Bench and Who&When show that VerifyMAS consistently improves diverse backbone models, including open-source Qwen and API-based GPT models, outperforming prior methods without sacrificing inference efficiency for long multi-agent trajectories.

</details>

Abstract: Nowadays, people in Bangladesh frequently rely on the internet and social media for daily news instead of traditional newspapers. However, the spread of false Bangla news through these platforms poses risks and challenges to the credibility of authentic media. Although several studies have been conducted on detecting Bangla fake news, there is still significant room for improvement in this area. To assist people, this research explores the effectiveness of feature selection approaches in identifying appropriate features, such as semantic, statistical, and character-level features, or their combinations, on the BanFakeNews-2.0 dataset for detecting Bangla fake news using a CNN model. In this paper, key findings reveal that combining multiple features significantly improves recall and F1-scores compared to using individual features alone. The code for this research can be availed here, https://github.com/gulzar09/Bn\_FNews\_H.Feature.

</details>


### [46] [Residual Semantic Decomposition of Word Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17482)
*Seungmin Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出残差语义分解（RSD），一种词嵌入的神经加性分解方法，在平衡嵌入重建和关系结构保留的同时，支持递归二元分解以提取局部语义轴，并通过残差暴露未被吸收的信息。实验表明RSD能够分离上下文锚点，但残差邻域更适合作为定性诊断工具而非基准语义预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有词嵌入方法难以在保持嵌入重建质量和关系结构保留之间取得平衡，且缺乏有效的分解手段来揭示嵌入空间中的局部语义结构。

Method: 提出残差语义分解（RSD），一种神经加性分解方法，通过递归二元分解（每次K=2）提取局部语义轴，残差保留未被当前轴吸收的信息，平衡重建损失与结构保留。

Result: 在人工标注的配对上下文诊断任务中，RSD能有效分离给定的上下文锚点，效果优于打乱标签的基线；但熵诊断显示歧义目标并非静态GloVe中的均匀高熵边界点。

Conclusion: 残差邻域更适合作为定性诊断工具来揭示嵌入空间的语义结构，而非用作基准级别的词义消歧预测。

Abstract: We introduce Residual Semantic Decomposition (RSD), a neural additive decomposition of word embeddings that balances embedding reconstruction with relational structure preservation. RSD supports recursive binary decomposition: each $K=2$ fit extracts a local semantic axis, while residuals expose information not absorbed by that axis. In manually specified paired-context diagnostics over ambiguous words, RSD separates supplied context anchors above shuffled-label controls, but entropy diagnostics show that ambiguous targets are not uniformly high-entropy boundary points in static GloVe. We therefore treat residual neighborhoods as qualitative diagnostics rather than benchmark sense predictions.

</details>


### [47] [Mixture of Experts for Low-Resource LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17598)
*Ori Bar Joseph,Smadar Arvatz,Noam Kayzer,Dan Revital,Sarel Weinberger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MoE模型在低资源语言（如希伯来语）中表现出深层路由崩溃现象，持续预训练可显著纠正此问题，且路由改善与下游性能提升相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索MoE架构中专家路由行为在资源不足语言（以形态丰富的希伯来语为例）中的表现，理解预训练代表性的不足如何影响路由动态。

Method: 对比分析两种不同架构的MoE模型（纯Transformer Qwen3-30B-A3B和混合Mamba-Transformer Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B），使用希伯来语作为低资源测试语言，结合持续预训练（CPT）和有监督微调（SFT）进行干预，并在日语上做跨语言验证。

Result: 两种预训练模型在深层均出现路由崩溃（使用熵急剧下降，token集中在少数专家）；平衡双语数据的CPT能显著纠正此不平衡，而单独SFT效果有限；日语中观察到定量一致的崩溃特征，验证了该现象的跨语言普适性；路由改善与下游基准测试提升相关。

Conclusion: 路由崩溃是预训练代表性不足的系统性后果，而非语言内在属性；路由熵和专家专业化可作为MoE系统多语言能力的诊断指标。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures enable efficient model scaling, yet expert routing behavior across underrepresented languages remains poorly understood. We analyze routing dynamics in two architecturally distinct MoE models -- a pure Transformer (Qwen3-30B-A3B) and a hybrid Mamba-Transformer (Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B) -- using Hebrew as a morphologically rich, low-resource testbed. Both pre-trained models exhibit \emph{deep-layer routing collapse}: usage entropy drops sharply in final layers and tokens concentrate on a narrow expert subset, a pattern largely absent for English. Continual pre-training (CPT) on balanced bilingual data substantially corrects this imbalance, increasing entropy and shifting routing toward shared, language-agnostic experts; supervised fine-tuning (SFT) alone achieves less complete correction. Extending the analysis to Japanese reveals quantitatively consistent collapse signatures, providing cross-linguistic evidence that the phenomenon is a systematic consequence of pre-training underrepresentation rather than any language-intrinsic property. Routing improvements correlate with consistent downstream benchmark gains, positioning routing entropy and expert specialization as principled diagnostics for multilingual capacity in MoE systems.

</details>


### [48] [Temporal Decay of Co-Citation Predictability: A 20-Year Statute Retrieval Benchmark from 396M Ukrainian Court Citations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17639)
*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过构建包含20年（2007-2026）乌克兰法律引文数据集，发现共引结构的预测能力随时间显著衰退（Adamic-Adar MRR下降33%-47%），且衰退不均衡：刑法稳定、民法因司法改革大幅下降，中频法条（1K-10K引用）预测性减半，文本基线（BM25）和嵌入漂移分析进一步验证了这种时序衰减机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证法学信息系统中长期以来的一个核心假设——共引结构能为法律检索提供稳定的信号——是否在时间维度上成立。

Method: 构建UA-StatuteRetrieval基准，包含2007-2026年20个年度快照、396百万法典引用、101百万乌克兰法院判决。采用留一法验证完整二分图，并设置固定文章集和训练/测试时间分裂两种评估协议，使用Adamic-Adar、BM25文本基线和E5-large嵌入漂移分析。

Result: Adamic-Adar MRR在固定文章集上下降33%（0.43→0.29），时间分裂协议下下降47%（0.51→0.27）。刑法稳定（MRR~0.40），民法因2017年司法改革从0.35降至0.15。中频法条（1K-10K引用）预测性减半，BM25下降31%，E5-large显示4.3%语义偏移。

Conclusion: 共引结构作为法律检索信号存在显著的时序衰减，非均匀分布，且与司法改革、引用语义漂移等机制相关，表明法律信息检索系统需考虑时间动态性。

Abstract: Co-citation structure is widely assumed to provide stable retrieval signal in legal information systems. We test this assumption longitudinally by constructing UA-StatuteRetrieval, a benchmark that measures co-citation predictability across 20 annual snapshots (2007-2026) of 396 million codex citations from 101 million Ukrainian court decisions. Using a leave-one-out protocol over the full bipartite citation graph, we find that Adamic-Adar MRR declines 33% on a fixed set of articles (from 0.43 to 0.29) and 47% under a train/test temporal split (from 0.51 to 0.27) confirming genuine temporal decay rather than compositional shift or evaluation artifact. The decay is non-uniform: criminal procedure maintains stable co-citation patterns (MRR ~0.40), while civil law degrades from 0.35 to 0.15, coinciding with the 2017 judicial reform. Hub articles (>100K citations) resist decay, but mid-frequency articles (1K-10K) -- the practical retrieval frontier lose half their predictability. A BM25 text baseline decays even faster (31%), and embedding drift analysis with E5-large reveals a 4.3% semantic shift in how articles are cited, providing a mechanistic explanation for the observed decay. The benchmark is released at https://huggingface.co/datasets/overthelex/ua-statute-retrieval.

</details>


### [49] [Monitoring the Internal Monologue: Probe Trajectories Reveal Reasoning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18549)
*Maciej Chrabąszcz,Aleksander Szymczyk,Marcin Sendera,Tomasz Trzciński,Sebastian Cygert*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过分析大型推理模型（LRM）的隐藏表示，提出利用探针轨迹（probe trajectory）——即推理过程中概念概率的连续演化——来预测模型未来行为。研究发现，完整轨迹比单点静态预测更能区分模型行为；通过提取波动性、趋势和稳态等信号处理特征，可显著提升对模型未来状态的区分能力。基于模板的训练数据可替代动态生成数据，且最大池化操作（AUROC达95%）远优于平均池化和最后token方法。在安全与数学领域的四个数据集和四个推理模型上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRM的思维链（CoT）推理为安全监控提供了新机会，但CoT并不总是忠实于模型的最终输出，这削弱了其作为监控工具的可靠性。因此，研究者希望从LRM的隐藏表示中寻找预测未来行为的方法，以弥补CoT不可靠的缺陷。

Method: 在每个生成token上部署探针（probe），构建探针轨迹（概念概率随推理过程的连续演化）；提取信号处理特征（波动性、趋势、稳态）来刻画时间动态；对比不同池化操作（平均池化、最后token、最大池化）和训练数据生成方式（模板 vs. 动态生成）的效果。

Result: 完整轨迹比单点静态预测更能区分模型未来行为；信号处理特征显著提升状态区分能力；模板训练数据与动态生成数据性能接近；最大池化达95% AUROC，而平均池化和最后token方法性能接近随机；在安全与数学领域四个数据集和四个推理模型上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 探针轨迹可作为监控LRM行为的补充框架，有效弥补CoT不忠实带来的监控可靠性问题。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) introduce new opportunities for safety monitoring through their Chain of Thought (CoT) reasoning. However, CoT is not always faithful to the model's final output, undermining its reliability as a monitoring tool. To address this, we investigate the hidden representations of LRMs to determine whether future behavior can be predicted from prompt and CoT representations. By evaluating a probe at each generated token, we construct a probe trajectory, the continuous evolution of a concept's probability across the reasoning process. We find that future model behavior is more distinguishable when examined over the full trajectory than from a single static prediction. To characterize these temporal dynamics, we extract signal-processing features that capture volatility, trend, and steady-state behavior, significantly improving the separation of future model states. We also present two methodological insights. First, template-based training data achieves near-parity with dynamically generated model responses, eliminating the need for a costly initial inference and labeling. Second, the choice of pooling operation is critical: average-pooling and last-token methods collapse to near-random performance, while max-pooling achieves up to 95% AUROC and yields stable probe trajectories. Using four datasets and four reasoning models across the domains of safety and mathematics, we demonstrate that trajectory features encode task-specific dynamics that improve outcome separability. These findings establish probe trajectories as a complementary framework for monitoring LRM behavior.
  Warning: This article contains potentially harmful content.

</details>


### [50] [Beyond Transcripts: Iterative Peer-Editing with Audio Unlocks High-Quality Human Summaries of Conversational Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17652)
*Kaavya Chaparala,Thomas Thebaud,Jesús Villalba López,Laureano Moro-Velazquez,Peter Viechnicki,Najim Dehak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在语音摘要任务中，使用人工标注者创建基准数据集时，不同标注工作流（输入模态、编辑方式）对摘要质量的影响，发现基于音频的摘要信息量较少，但通过迭代同伴编辑可以弥补这一差距，使其与基于文本的摘要及LLM摘要相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前语音摘要任务缺乏成熟的基准数据集，而创建新基准需要人工标注，因为LLM可能将系统性错误和偏见引入数据集。因此需要探索高质量的人工标注工作流。

Method: 测试了十种标注工作流，包括三种输入模态（音频、转录文本、两者兼有）以及是否包含编辑（自编辑或同伴编辑）。比较了人工基于音频的摘要与基于转录文本的摘要，并将人工输出与四种LLM基准（三种文本、一种音频）进行对比。

Result: 基于音频的摘要信息量较少且更压缩；但迭代同伴编辑结合音频输入可缓解这一差异，使基于音频的摘要达到与基于转录文本的摘要和LLM摘要相当的信息量。

Conclusion: 验证了人工标注者之间的迭代同伴编辑可用于创建同时包含词汇和韵律信息的基准数据集，即使在转录文本不可用的情况下也能进行关键的数据集收集。

Abstract: There are not enough established benchmarks for the task fo speech summarization. Creating new benchmarks demands human annotation, as LLMs could embed systemic errors and bias into datasets. We test ten annotation workflows varying input modality (audio, transcript, or both) and the inclusion of editing (self or peer-editing) to investigate potential quality tradeoffs from using human annotators to summarize audio. We compare human audio-based summaries to human transcript-based summaries to track the impact of the different information modalities on summary quality. We also compare the human outputs against four LLM benchmarks (three text, one audio) to examine whether human-written summaries are less informative than highly fluent automated outputs. We find that audio-based summaries are less informative and more compressed than transcript summaries. However, iterative peer-editing with audio mitigates this difference, enabling audio-based summaries to be as informative as their transcript counterparts and LLM summaries. These findings validate iterative peer-editing among human annotators for the creation of benchmarks informed by both lexical and prosodic information. This enables crucial dataset collection even in setting where transcripts are unavailable.

</details>


### [51] [Stop When Reasoning Converges: Semantic-Preserving Early Exit for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17672)
*Dehai Min,Giovanni Vaccarino,Huiyi Chen,Yongliang Wu,Gal Yona,Lu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PUMA是一个即插即用的推理提前退出框架，通过检测推理层面的语义冗余（而非仅依赖答案层面信号）来判断推理是否收敛，结合轻量级冗余检测器与答案级验证，在保持答案准确性和推理链完整性的同时，平均减少26.2%的token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型推理模型（LRMs）在生成长思维链时存在"过度思考"问题，即在答案已稳定后继续推理，浪费token并增加延迟。已有的推理时提前退出方法主要依赖答案级信号（如置信度或答案一致性）来判断何时停止，但这些信号反映的是答案就绪度而非推理收敛性，可能导致模型在探索或自我修正尚未完成时过早退出，降低最终答案准确性并使保留的推理链语义不完整。

Method: 提出PUMA框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 轻量级冗余检测器（Redundancy Detector），用于标记语义冗余的候选退出点，即当连续推理步骤不再增加新进展而是重复已有结论时进行检测；(2) 答案级验证模块，确认停止是否安全。两者协同工作，在移除冗余推理的同时保持答案准确性和连贯的推理前缀。该框架为即插即用设计，可适配不同LRM。

Result: 在5种LRM和5个具有挑战性的推理基准上，PUMA平均减少26.2%的token消耗，同时保持准确率和保留的思维链质量。额外实验表明，推理级冗余在代码生成、零样本视觉-语言推理以及学习停止策略内化等场景中均表现出鲁棒性、可迁移性和可学习性。

Conclusion: 推理层面的语义冗余是语义保持型提前退出的有效互补信号，PUMA成功利用该信号实现了高效且准确的推理，且该方法具有跨任务、跨模型的可迁移性以及可内化为学习策略的潜力。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong performance by generating long chains of thought (CoT), but often overthink, continuing to reason after a solution has already stabilized and thereby wasting tokens and increasing latency. Existing inference-time early-exit methods rely primarily on answer-level signals, such as confidence or trial-answer consistency, to decide when to stop. However, these signals mainly reflect answer readiness rather than reasoning convergence: they may trigger before the model has finished exploring or self-correcting, causing premature exits that can degrade final-answer accuracy and leave the retained reasoning chain semantically incomplete. We identify reasoning-level semantic redundancy as a complementary signal for semantic-preserving early exit: when successive steps no longer add novel progress and instead revisit established conclusions, the reasoning trajectory has likely converged. Building on this insight, we propose PUMA, a plug-and-play framework that combines a lightweight Redundancy Detector with answer-level verification. The detector flags semantically redundant candidate exits, while verification confirms whether stopping is safe, allowing PUMA to remove redundant continuation while preserving both answer accuracy and a coherent reasoning prefix. Across five LRMs and five challenging reasoning benchmarks, PUMA achieves 26.2% average token reduction while preserving accuracy and retained CoT quality. Additional experiments on code generation, zero-shot vision-language reasoning, and learned stopping-policy internalization further demonstrate that reasoning-level redundancy is a robust, transferable, and learnable signal for efficient reasoning. Our code is available at \url{https://github.com/giovanni-vaccarino/PUMA}.

</details>


### [52] [Validate Your Authority: Benchmarking LLMs on Multi-Label Precedent Treatment Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17691)
*M. Mikail Demir,M. Abdullah Canbaz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对法律先例中负面处理分类这一高风险NLP任务，提出更鲁棒的评估框架。使用239条专家标注的真实法律引文数据集，提出平均严重度误差（Average Severity Error）指标，对现代大语言模型进行基准测试。结果显示：Gemini 2.5 Flash在高层次分类任务中准确率最高（79.1%），GPT-5-mini在细粒度分类中表现最佳（67.7%）。研究建立了关键基线，提供了新数据集和定制化评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律先例中负面处理的自动分类是高风险NLP任务，标准准确率指标存在缺陷，无法有效衡量实际场景中分类错误的严重程度，亟需更鲁棒的评估框架。

Method: 构建了239条专家标注的真实法律引文数据集；提出平均严重度误差（Average Severity Error）指标；对多个现代大语言模型进行基准测试，分别在高层级和细粒度分类任务上评估。

Result: Gemini 2.5 Flash在高层次分类任务中最高准确率79.1%；GPT-5-mini在细粒度分类任务中最高准确率67.7%。不同模型在不同粒度任务上表现存在分化。

Conclusion: 本研究为法律文本中负面处理分类建立了关键基线，提供了新的上下文丰富数据集，并引入了针对复杂法律推理任务定制的评估指标，为后续研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Automating the classification of negative treatment in legal precedent is a critical yet nuanced NLP task where misclassification carries significant risk. To address the shortcomings of standard accuracy, this paper introduces a more robust evaluation framework. We benchmark modern Large Language Models on a new, expert-annotated dataset of 239 real-world legal citations and propose a novel Average Severity Error metric to better measure the practical impact of classification errors. Our experiments reveal a performance split. Google's Gemini 2.5 Flash achieved the highest accuracy on a high-level classification task (79.1%), while OpenAI's GPT-5-mini was the top performer on the more complex fine-grained schema (67.7%). This work establishes a crucial baseline, provides a new context-rich dataset, and introduces an evaluation metric tailored to the demands of this complex legal reasoning task.

</details>


### [53] [Do LLM Agents Mirror Socio-Cognitive Effects in Power-Asymmetric Conversations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17694)
*Anvesh Rao Vijjini,Sagar Manjunath,Snigdha Chaturvedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）在被赋予高低地位角色时，是否表现出与人类类似的权力差异引发的社会认知效应，包括语言协调、代词使用、说服成功率和有害服从行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权力差异通过社会认知效应塑造人类沟通，包括语言协调、代词使用、权威偏见和有害服从。作者想探究LLMs在被赋予不同地位角色时是否也会表现出类似的行为模式。

Method: 使用来自不同职业的角色，模拟多轮、权力不对称的对话（如校长-教师、法官-律师），测量四种指标：（i）语言协调程度，（ii）代词使用模式，（iii）说服成功率，（iv）对不安全请求的服从程度。

Result: LLMs表现出权力的关键社会认知效应，但存在细微差别和变异性，模拟互动导致了既包含理想行为也包含不安全行为的结果。

Conclusion: LLMs在角色扮演中能够复现人类权力差异的社会认知效应，这既带来了有益的对话协调，也可能导致对不安全请求的服从等有害行为。

Abstract: Power differences shape human communication through well documented socio cognitive effects, including language coordination, pronoun usage, authority bias, and harmful compliance. We examine whether large language models (LLMs) exhibit similar behaviors when assigned high or low status personas. Using personas from diverse professions, we simulate multi turn, power asymmetric dialogues (e.g., principal teacher, justice lawyer) and measure (i) linguistic coordination, (ii) pronoun usage, (iii) persuasion success, and (iv) compliance with unsafe requests. Our results show that LLMs show key socio cognitive effects of power, albeit with nuances and variability, linking simulated interactions to both desirable and unsafe behaviors.

</details>


### [54] [Sometin Beta Pass Notin (SBPN): Improving Multilingual ASR for Nigerian Languages via Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17710)
*Sewade Ogun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段蒸馏框架（知识蒸馏+伪标签自迭代）构建多语言ASR模型SBPN，覆盖五种尼日利亚语言，以29%的相对WER降低显著缩小了与高资源语言的性能差距，并开源了120M/600M参数的基座模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尼日利亚语言在多语言ASR系统中性能显著落后于英语、法语等高资源语言，面临数据稀缺、正字法不一致、声调符号、口音多样、频繁语码转换和本地化命名实体等独特建模挑战。

Method: 两阶段蒸馏流程：第一阶段利用学生-教师知识蒸馏，以现有单语模型为教师，结合语言特定的N-gram语言模型进行条件约束；第二阶段使用伪标注数据迭代自训练，进一步优化精度。

Result: 在Common Voice和Fleurs等基准上，该方法平均相对WER降低29%，超越了单语基线及当前最先进的多语模型。发布SBPN-Base（120M参数）和SBPN-Large（600M参数）两个开源模型。

Conclusion: 两阶段蒸馏策略有效弥合了尼日利亚低资源语言与高资源语言之间的性能差距，开源模型为后续相关语音和文化研究提供了基础资源。

Abstract: Although modern multilingual Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) systems support several Nigerian languages, their performance consistently lags behind high-resource languages like English and French. Nigerian languages present unique modelling hurdles, including acute data scarcity, inconsistent orthography, tonal diacritics, diverse accents, frequent code-switching, and localized named entities. To address these challenges, we developed a multilingual ASR framework utilizing a two-stage distillation process. First, we employ student-teacher knowledge distillation from existing monolingual models, conditioned on robust language-specific N-gram language models. Second, we perform iterative self improvement using pseudo-labelled data to further refine accuracy. Our method significantly bridges the performance gap, achieving on average a relative Word Error Rate (WER) reduction of 29 % over monolingual baselines. Our models also outperform state-of-the-art multilingual models across major benchmarks, including Common Voice and Fleurs. We introduce Sometin Beta Pass Notin (SBPN), a foundational multilingual ASR model covering Yorùbá, Hausa, Igbo, Nigerian Pidgin, and Nigerian English. SBPN is released in two sizes: SBPN-Base (120 M parameters) and SBPN-Large (600 M parameters). By releasing these as open foundation models, we aim to provide ASR resources for further research into the rich phonetic and cultural landscape of the region.

</details>
