The discovery phase revealed systemic challenges, including distributed cryptographic ownership, uneven evidence quality across legacy and modern environments, and high dependency on third party cryptographic roadmaps. To operationalise these findings, the organisation introduced a structured exposure register that enabled prioritisation based on asset criticality, confidentiality longevity, and migration feasibility. We argue that PQC discovery should be understood as a governance capability that stabilises organisational knowledge and converts cryptographic uncertainty into measurable accountability, supporting risk based decision making and ecosystem coordination. The results contribute actionable lessons for institutions pursuing crypto-agility and resilience under post quantum harvest now, decrypt later threat models.

</details>


### [200] [A Method for Securely Transmitting Large Video Files Using Chaotic Compression and Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16563)
*Shiladitya Bhattacharjee,Subha Bhattacharya,Arnab Chatterjee,Sulabh Bansal,Saurabh Shukla*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合混沌映射加密与霍夫曼无损压缩的同步数据压缩加密系统(SDCE)，专为大视频文件设计，在减少计算开销的同时增强数据安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统压缩和加密技术繁琐且资源密集，不适用于实时应用，现有研究未能提出合适的联合策略。

Method: 将基于Logistic混沌映射的XOR加密与霍夫曼无损压缩融合到一个统一框架中，利用混沌序列产生伪随机密钥实现加密。

Result: 相比现有方法，在熵值、雪崩效应、吞吐量、压缩比、PSNR、BPC和数据丢失率等指标上均表现更优。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效增强数据隐私性和完整性，适用于大视频文件的实时处理场景。

Abstract: Conventional techniques for compression and encryption are frequently laborious and resource-intensive, rendering them inappropriate for real-time applications. A plethora of research has been presented in the current literature to address these difficulties together; yet, it fails to propose any suitable strategy. Therefore, this study introduces an innovative simultaneous data compression and encryption (SDCE) system specifically designed for large video files. The methodology amalgamates chaotic map-based encryption with Huffman encoding for lossless compression into a cohesive framework, markedly diminishing computational overhead and processing duration while augmenting data security. The logistic map is utilized to produce a pseudo-random chaotic sequence for XOR-based encryption, guaranteeing robust security against unwanted access. The research findings demonstrate its efficacy in enhancing data privacy compared to other existing and related strategies, particularly in terms of generating greater entropy and avalanche effects. It produces superior throughput, compression ratio, peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR), and reduced bits per rate (BPC), along with a smaller percentage of data loss, which further supports its ability to provide enhanced data integrity compared to other existing methods.

</details>


### [201] [SLEIGHT-Bench: A Benchmark of Evasion Attacks Against Agent Monitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16626)
*Elle Najt,Colin Toft,Tyler Tracy,Fabien Roger,Joe Benton*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SLEIGHT-Bench是一个包含40种攻击（11个类别）的基准测试集，用于评估LLM监控器检测编码智能体隐蔽有害行为的能力。研究发现，Opus 4.6监控器在1%假阳性率下仅能捕获23%的攻击，24/40的攻击从未被捕获，并识别出多种绕过策略。使用编码智能体作为监控器或针对性提示可提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主编码智能体可能产生复杂且危险的行为，使用LLM作为监控器来降低风险是直观思路，但需系统评估监控器面对多样化攻击策略的局限性。

Method: 构建包含40种攻击（11个类别）的合成Transcript基准测试集，攻击涉及权重窃取、凭证盗窃、未监控恶意部署等有害目标。对Opus 4.6监控器在1%假阳性率下进行10轮测试，并对比不同监控器配置（编码智能体作为监控器 vs. 常规提示监控器）及针对性提示的效果。

Result: 40种攻击中24种从未被捕获，整体捕获率仅23%。识别出利用模型先验、用户指令歧义、状态操纵等绕过策略。编码智能体作为监控器比常规提示监控器表现更强，针对性提示可提升部分策略的捕获率。

Conclusion: 当前前沿LLM监控器在检测编码智能体隐蔽有害行为方面存在显著局限，需要更强大的监控机制来应对多样化的攻击策略。

Abstract: Since autonomous coding agents generate complex behaviors at high-volume, we may want to use other LLMs to monitor actions to reduce the risk from dangerous misaligned behavior. To better understand the limitations of such monitors against the diverse attack strategies that a coding agent could use, we present SLEIGHT-Bench (Subtle Low-itEration Insight-Guided Harmful Transcripts), a benchmark of synthetic transcripts containing 40 attacks across 11 categories, each showing a coding agent covertly pursuing a harmful objective (e.g. weight exfiltration, credential theft, unmonitored rogue deployment). We find that 24 of 40 attacks are never caught by an Opus 4.6 monitor with extended thinking across 10 trials at a 1% false-positive rate, while the overall catch rate is 23%. We identify several evasion strategies such as exploiting model priors, user instruction ambiguity, and state manipulation, which facilitate creating attacks that evade frontier monitors. We also elicit stronger monitor performance using coding agents as monitors versus regular prompted monitors, and for some evasion strategies show improved catch rates with targeted monitor prompts. Our dataset and evaluation framework are available at https://github.com/safety-research/sleight-bench and https://huggingface.co/datasets/sleightbench/SLEIGHT-Bench.

</details>


### [202] [Stop Starving or Stuffing Me: Boosting Firmware Fuzzing Efficiency with On-demand Input Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16798)
*Shandian Shen,Wei Zhou,Keming Zhao,Peng Liu,Chung Hwan Kim,Le Guan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 固件模糊测试中现有方法采用临时性输入投递方式（过量填充或投递不足），导致测试能力受限。本文提出FIDO，通过静态和动态分析将输入处理路径映射为三个阶段（输入检索、可用性检查、处理），从而在可用性检查点精确投递输入，并优化多输入路径的调度算法。实验表明，FIDO作为现有固件模糊测试工具的插件，中位数代码覆盖率提升最高达115%，并发现5个未知漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固件输入具有异步性和不确定性（时机和数量不可控），而现有模糊测试工具将通用软件的输入投递方式直接套用于固件，导致填充过量（stuffing）或投递不足（starving）问题，严重削弱了模糊测试能力。

Method: 通过静态和动态分析将每条输入处理路径划分为三个阶段：输入检索、可用性检查和处理。在可用性检查点以期望长度范围精确投递测试用例，并采用优化调度算法覆盖更多多样化的输入路径。

Result: 作为插件集成到现有固件模糊测试工具中，相比Fuzzware和MULTIFUZZ的临时性投递方法，中位数代码覆盖率分别提升115%和54%；相比需要人工指定投递点的SEmu，覆盖率提升19%；显著加快已知漏洞发现速度，并挖掘出5个先前未知的漏洞。

Conclusion: 通过语义感知的输入投递机制（利用固件处理输入前必须检查可用性的特性），FIDO有效解决了固件模糊测试中的输入投递难题，提升了代码覆盖率和漏洞发现效率，可作为增强模块推广使用。

Abstract: Firmware fuzzing has gained attention for identifying firmware bugs. However, current approaches often directly integrate fuzzing tools for general software. General software receives input as it encounters I/O functions, but firmware input can be received asynchronously and independently of the firmware's execution, with uncertain timing and quantity. Without full awareness of firmware's exceptions, existing solutions often imprudently deliver fuzzer-generated input to the firmware in an ad-hoc way. This either overwhelms the processing function of the firmware (stuffing) or fails to deliver enough input data to trigger input processing functions (starving). In both cases, fuzzing capability is weakened.
  In this paper, we comprehensively investigate the input delivery issue. To determine the optimal timing and quantity for delivering test cases, we leverage the fact that firmware has to check input availability before using data. So we employ static and dynamic analysis to map each input processing route into three stages: input retrieval, availability check, and processing. This recovered semantic information allows the fuzzer to accurately deliver input at the availability check points within the expected length range. For multiple input routes problem, we also optimize the scheduling algorithm to reach more diverse routes. Our prototype, named FIDO, can serve as an add-on to existing firmware fuzzers to enhance their test-case delivery effectiveness. Compared to ad-hoc input delivery methods used in Fuzzware and MULTIFUZZ, FIDO increases their median code coverage by up to 115% and 54%, respectively. Compared to SEmu, which requires humans to manually specify input delivery points, FIDO still improves its coverage by up to 19%. As a result, FIDO discovers known bugs significantly faster and also identifies five previously unknown bugs.

</details>


### [203] [Universal Graph Backdoor Defense: A Feature-based Homophily Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16815)
*Mengting Pan,Fan Li,Chen Chen,Xiaoyang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次研究通用图后门防御问题，从基于特征的同质性视角分析了两种类型后门攻击的共同效应，并提出利用节点级局部特征一致性（通过邻域感知重构损失建模）来区分后门节点与干净节点，结合鲁棒训练策略消除触发器影响，实验证明该方法在子图型和特征型攻击下均能显著降低攻击成功率并保持清洁准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图后门防御主要针对子图型后门攻击，依赖中毒目标节点与子图触发器显式连接的假设，无法防御新兴的保持图拓扑结构的特征型后门攻击。因此需要研究通用的图后门防御方法。

Method: 首先从基于特征的同质性视角研究两种攻击类型的共同效应，通过理论和实证分析发现后门节点比干净节点表现出更低的特征同质性。基于此，提出利用节点级局部特征一致性（由邻域感知重构损失建模）区分后门节点与干净节点，并设计鲁棒训练策略消除触发器影响同时减少检测不确定性引入的噪声。

Result: 大量实验证明，该框架在子图型和特征型攻击下均能显著降低攻击成功率，同时保持具有竞争力的清洁准确性。

Conclusion: 基于特征同质性视角的通用图后门防御方法有效，能够同时应对子图型和特征型图后门攻击。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) have achieved remarkable success in relational learning. However, their vulnerability to graph backdoor attacks (GBAs) poses a significant barrier to broader adoption in high-stakes applications. Despite recent advances in graph backdoor defense (GBD), existing methods primarily focus on subgraph-based GBAs, relying on the assumption that poisoned target nodes are explicitly connected to subgraph triggers. Our empirical results reveal that such structure-centric approaches fail to defend against emerging feature-based GBAs that preserve graph topology. Therefore, in this paper, we study a novel problem of universal graph backdoor defense. First, we investigate the shared effects of both attack types from a feature-based homophily perspective, which characterizes local feature consistency between nodes and their neighborhoods. Thorough theoretical and empirical analyses demonstrate that, regardless of trigger mechanisms, backdoors induced by GBAs exhibit lower feature-based homophily than clean nodes, indicating a discrepancy in local feature similarity. Motivated by this insight, we propose to leverage node-level local feature consistency, modeled by a neighbor-aware reconstruction loss, to distinguish backdoors from clean nodes. Then, a robust training strategy is developed to eliminate trigger effects while reducing noise induced by detection uncertainty. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework significantly degrades the attack success rate and maintains competitive clean accuracy under both subgraph-based and feature-based attacks.

</details>


### [204] [A Lightweight QR-assisted Zero-knowledge Identification Protocol For Secure Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16912)
*Hüseyin Bodur*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于QR码的轻量级零知识认证模型，采用Schnorr协议，通过nonce和时间戳机制防止重放攻击，证明数据嵌入QR码传输，在256位安全级别下生成和验证时间达毫秒级，证明大小恒定约0.5KB，适用于移动和低资源系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零知识认证方案在移动和低资源设备上计算开销大，且缺乏对QR码作为传输介质的轻量级支持，需要一种兼顾安全性与性能的轻量化认证方案。

Method: 基于Schnorr认证协议，引入nonce和时间戳机制增强抗重放攻击能力；将证明者生成的零知识证明数据嵌入QR码，通过QR码传输至验证者完成验证，确保不泄露密钥信息。

Result: 在256位安全级别下，证明生成和验证时间均为毫秒级；证明大小恒定约0.5 KB，满足QR码容量限制；仿真结果验证了方案在安全性和性能上的可行性。

Conclusion: 该轻量级零知识认证模型在安全性和性能上均表现良好，适用于移动设备和低资源系统的实际应用场景。

Abstract: This study proposes a lightweight Zero-Knowledge authentication model supported by QR codes. The approach is based on the Schnorr authentication protocol and provides an additional security layer against replay attacks through nonce and timestamp mechanisms. The proof data generated by the prover is embedded within a QR code and transmitted to the verifier. Thus, the system enables verification of knowledge of the secret key without revealing it. Simulation results show that proof generation and verification times under a 256-bit security level are in the millisecond range. Additionally, the proof size remains constant at approximately 0.5 KB, making it suitable for practical applications in terms of QR code capacity. The findings indicate that the proposed model is applicable in mobile and low-resource systems in terms of both security and performance.

</details>


### [205] [Securing LLM Agents Need Intent-to-Execution Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16976)
*Wenjie Qu,Ming Xu,Peiran Wang,Shengfang Zhai,Jiaheng Zhang,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出，保障LLM智能体安全需要定义端到端的正确性属性——意图到执行完整性（Intent-to-Execution Integrity），该属性由工具完整性、指令完整性、判断完整性和数据流完整性四个子属性构成。通过编译器类比，揭示现有防御仅提供部分且不可组合的覆盖，存在根本性安全缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体防御隐式假设工具可信，但OpenClaw等系统引入第三方技能和直接环境访问，打破了这一假设。目前缺乏定义"安全"的正确性属性，也无法系统评估已有防御的覆盖范围。

Method: 通过将LLM智能体与编译器进行结构类比，识别出两类根本问题来源（不可信数据摄入和不可信工具执行），推导出四个必须同时满足的完整性属性，并据此分析现有防御机制的覆盖情况。

Result: 现有智能体防御系统仅提供部分且不可组合的覆盖，在工具完整性、指令完整性、判断完整性和数据流完整性四个维度上均存在根本性安全缺口。

Conclusion: 保障LLM智能体安全需要以意图到执行完整性为核心正确性属性，当前防御体系尚无法满足这一要求，亟需设计能够同时满足四个完整性属性的端到端安全方案。

Abstract: This position paper argues that securing LLM agents requires first defining an end-to-end correctness property that specifies when an agent's execution faithfully reflects the user's intent. Modern LLM agents operate over an \emph{intent-to-execution pipeline}, where natural-language instructions are translated into concrete system operations such as tool calls, API requests, and code execution. While recent defenses have made progress in constraining how agents construct tool calls, most existing formulations implicitly assume that tools are trusted. The emergence of systems such as OpenClaw, with open ecosystems of third-party skills and direct access to user environments, breaks this assumption and exposes new failure modes, including malicious or over-privileged components in the execution pipeline.
  Despite rapid progress in defense mechanisms, there is no adequate correctness property that defines what ``secure'' means for LLM agents, nor a principled way to evaluate the coverage of existing defenses. We observe that LLM agents are structurally analogous to compilers, where security violations correspond to mis-executions that do not preserve user intent. Drawing on this analogy, we identify two fundamental problem sources -- untrusted data ingestion and untrusted tool execution -- and derive four integrity properties that must hold simultaneously: \emph{Tool Integrity}, \emph{Instruction Integrity}, \emph{Judgment Integrity}, and \emph{Data Flow Integrity}. We call their conjunction \emph{intent-to-execution integrity}.
  Analyzing existing agentic defenses against these properties reveals that current systems provide only partial and non-compositional coverage, leaving fundamental gaps in securing modern LLM agents.

</details>


### [206] [The Range Shrinks, the Threat Remains: Re-evaluating LLM Package Hallucinations on the 2026 Frontier-Model Cohort](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17062)
*Aleksandr Churilov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文复现了Spracklen等人关于代码生成LLM幻觉包名的研究，评估了五个前沿模型（2025.10-2026.03发布），发现整体幻觉率在4.62%-6.10%之间，模型间差异大幅缩小但威胁未消除；识别出127个所有模型一致虚构的包名，构成模型无关的供应链攻击面；并发现了多种幻觉不对称性和模型间相似性模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Spracklen等人发现的幻觉包名安全威胁是否在最新一代代码LLM中仍然存在，并进一步揭示单模型研究无法发现的跨模型一致性的供应链攻击面。

Method: 在199,845对Python和JavaScript提示上，对Claude Sonnet 4.6、Claude Haiku 4.5、GPT-5.4-mini、Gemini 2.5 Pro和DeepSeek V3.2五个模型进行系统评估，将生成的包名与PyPI和npm主列表验证；通过跨模型比较识别共同虚构的包名，并分析幻觉不对称性及模型间Jaccard相似性。

Result: 整体幻觉率4.62%-6.10%，模型间差异较Spracklen研究大幅压缩；识别出127个所有模型一致虚构的包名（109个PyPI，18个npm）；发现Python幻觉率高于JavaScript（与Spracklen 2024结果相反）；Claude Haiku 4.5幻觉率低于Sonnet 4.6（家族内反转）；DeepSeek V3.2与GPT-5.4-mini的Jaccard相似性最高（J=0.343），暗示共享训练数据来源。

Conclusion: 虽然最新模型的幻觉率差异显著缩小，但slopsquatting攻击威胁仍然存在；跨模型一致的虚构包名构成了更大的供应链安全风险；训练数据的同源性可能加剧这一问题，需要更全面的防御策略。

Abstract: Spracklen et al. (USENIX Security '25) showed that code-generating large language models hallucinate package names that do not exist on PyPI or npm at rates ranging from 5.2% on commercial models to 21.7% on open-source models, creating an attack surface for slopsquatting -- the registration of malicious packages under hallucinated names. We replicate their methodology on five frontier code-capable LLMs released between October 2025 and March 2026: Claude Sonnet 4.6, Claude Haiku 4.5, GPT-5.4-mini, Gemini 2.5 Pro, and DeepSeek V3.2. Across 199,845 paired Python and JavaScript prompts validated against PyPI and npm master lists, we measure overall hallucination rates between 4.62% (Claude Haiku 4.5) and 6.10% (GPT-5.4-mini) -- an order-of-magnitude compression of the inter-model spread observed by Spracklen, but not a retirement of the threat. Beyond replication, we identify a set of 127 package names (109 on PyPI, 18 on npm) that all five evaluated models invent identically, constituting a model-agnostic supply-chain attack surface that no single-model study can reveal. We further document a Python-over-JavaScript hallucination asymmetry that inverts Spracklen's 2024 finding, identify a Haiku-below-Sonnet inversion within the Anthropic family, and observe a Jaccard-similarity peak between DeepSeek V3.2 and GPT-5.4-mini (J = 0.343) suggestive of shared training-data origins.

</details>


### [207] [Simple Power Analysis on Post-Quantum Code Based Cryptosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17116)
*Konstantinos Spalas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过简单功耗分析（SPA）方法，评估了后量子密码方案McEliece和BIKE在解封装阶段的侧信道信息泄漏。实验表明，电磁辐射与秘密值存在相关性，仅用200条功耗轨迹即可通过机器学习模型预测共享会话密钥的秘密比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子密码方案（如McEliece和BIKE）虽能抵抗量子计算攻击，但其在实际硬件实现中的侧信道安全性尚未充分评估，特别是解封装阶段共享密钥生成过程中的信息泄漏风险。

Method: 使用低成本设备采集功耗轨迹，采用简单功耗分析（SPA）方法，分析电磁辐射与秘密值的相关性，并利用机器学习模型对收集到的200条功耗轨迹进行训练和预测。

Result: 电磁辐射与秘密值之间存在显著相关性；仅需200条功耗轨迹，机器学习模型即可成功预测解封装阶段产生的共享会话密钥中的秘密比特。

Conclusion: 基于编码理论的后量子密码方案（McEliece和BIKE）在硬件实现中存在侧信道信息泄漏风险，简单的功耗分析结合机器学习即可破解密钥，需进一步采取防护措施。

Abstract: Post-Quantum cryptography is about to substitute current cryptographic schemes as being resilient in attacks from quantum computers. McEleiece and Bit Flip Key Encapsulation (BIKE) are two delight representatives based on coding theory where classical structural attacks against these algorithms can be successfully phased out by selecting the appropriate key size. Using low cost equipment, the method of Simple Power Analysis (SPA) is used in this paper to evaluate whether or not there is significant information leakage during the decapsulation phase where the shared secret key is generated. Executing a related experiment it is shown that correlation between electromagnetic emissions and secret values exists. In the aftermath, with only 200 power traces collected, machine learning models can predict secret bits of the shared session key, produced during the decapsulation.

</details>


### [208] [New Wide-Net-Casting Jailbreak Attacks Risk Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17128)
*Qiuchi Xiang,Haoxuan Qu,Hossein Rahmani,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现并分析了一种新的越狱攻击场景——广域网投放场景（wide-net-casting scenario），攻击者可同时查询一组大模型以诱导有害输出，并针对该场景开发了特制越狱方法，在不设额外防护的情况下攻击成功率可达100%，揭示了该场景的高风险性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱攻击研究主要关注针对单个模型的攻击，但实际中攻击者可以同时查询多个模型，这一广域网投放场景的安全风险尚未被探索。

Method: 识别并定义广域网投放场景，分析其安全风险，并开发针对该场景的特制越狱攻击方法。

Result: 在无额外安全防护的大模型上，所提越狱方法攻击成功率可达100%，验证了广域网投放场景的高危性。

Conclusion: 广域网投放是一个独特且高风险的安全场景，亟需在未来评估和防御研究中予以关注。

Abstract: Jailbreak attacks on large models have drawn growing attention due to their close ties to societal safety. This work identifies a practical yet unexplored jailbreak scenario, the wide-net-casting scenario, where an adversary can query a group of large models instead of a single one to elicit harmful outputs. Our analysis reveals substantial yet previously overlooked safety risks under this scenario. As a key part of our analysis, we further develop a novel jailbreak method tailored to the wide-net-casting scenario. With this tailored method, the jailbreak success rate can even reach 100\% in some experiments when targeting the large models without additional safeguards, exposing wide-net-casting as a distinct, high-risk scenario that warrants attention in future evaluation and defense research.

</details>


### [209] [When Efficiency Backfires: Cascading LLMs Trigger Cascade Failure under Adversarial Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17288)
*Zehan Sun,Dingfan Chen,Songze Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次证明LLM级联系统易受针对性对抗性操纵攻击，通过约束顺序协同优化对抗后缀，可同时利用轻量模型和决策机制，导致成本效益和准确性可控下降，实验验证了攻击的实用性和严重性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM级联系统虽能平衡效率与性能，但其引入的新攻击面（轻量前端模型和内部决策机制）存在安全漏洞，而现有研究尚未对此进行探索。

Method: 提出一种新颖的攻击框架，采用约束顺序协同优化对抗后缀，利用级联依赖关系，同时攻击轻量模型和决策机制，可适应不同能力的攻击者，实现成本效益和准确性的可控降级。

Result: 在多种数据集和代表性LLM级联系统上的广泛实验验证了该攻击的实用性和严重性，其影响显著强于针对独立模型的攻击。

Conclusion: LLM级联系统的安全性亟需严格审查，需要更广泛关注此类设计中固有的系统性风险。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) cascade systems are designed to balance efficiency and performance by processing queries with lightweight models while selectively escalating complex cases to more powerful ones. Such systems seek to reduces computational cost and latency while maintaining task performance, making it an appealing choice for large-scale deployment. However, the cascade design introduces new vulnerabilities through an expanded attack surface: the inclusion of lightweight front-end models and internal decision mechanisms introduces new weaknesses. In this work, we present the first study demonstrating that LLM cascade systems are susceptible to targeted adversarial manipulation, which disrupts both performance objectives and the intended cost advantages of the cascade design. We propose a novel attack framework that employs constrained sequential collaborative optimization of adversarial suffix under cascade dependencies, enabling simultaneous exploitation of lightweight models and decision mechanisms. This framework adapts to adversaries with varying capabilities, inducing controllable degradation in both cost-efficiency and accuracy. Unlike prior attacks targeting standalone models, our approach strategically leverages the cascade structure to achieve significantly stronger impact. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets and representative LLM cascade systems validate the practicality and severity of this attack. Our findings highlight the urgent need to rigorously scrutinize the security of LLM cascade systems and call for broader attention to the systemic risks inherent in such designs.

</details>


### [210] [ASPI: Seeking Ambiguity Clarification Amplifies Prompt Injection Vulnerability in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17324)
*Udari Madhushani Sehwag,Zhengyang Shan,Heming Liu,Dileepa Lakshan,Joseph Brandifino,Max Fenkell*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ASPI基准测试，发现LLM智能体从标准执行状态切换到澄清询问状态时，对提示注入攻击的脆弱性显著增加（例如o3从1.8%升至34.0%），表明标准执行环境下的安全评估会系统性地低估交互式智能体的攻击面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清询问行为被视为LLM智能体的理想特性，但该交互模式的安全隐患尚未被探索。作者旨在研究从标准执行到澄清询问的状态转换是否会增加智能体对提示注入攻击的脆弱性。

Method: 构建ASPI基准测试（728个任务-攻击场景），将澄清定义为独立智能体状态，在匹配的执行和澄清设置下进行对照实验。执行设置中智能体基于完整指令行动，仅通过工具返回数据接触对抗内容；澄清设置中智能体需先请求并整合额外用户输入。评估10个前沿LLM。

Result: 澄清询问一致且显著地放大了脆弱性：o3攻击成功率从1.8%升至34.0%，Gemini-3-Flash从2.2%升至35.7%。分解分析表明，这一差距既源于模型处理传入内容时的状态依赖性转变，也源于智能体主动征询的澄清界面带来的通道特定效应。

Conclusion: 标准执行时的安全性评估会系统性低估交互式智能体的攻击面，在完全指定任务下的鲁棒性并不能转化为模糊任务下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Clarification-seeking behavior is widely regarded as a desirable property of LLM agents, enabling them to resolve ambiguity before acting on underspecified tasks. However, the security implications of this interaction pattern remain unexplored. We investigate whether the transition from standard execution to a clarification-seeking state increases an agent's susceptibility to prompt injection attacks. We introduce ASPI (Ambiguous-State Prompt Injection), a benchmark of 728 task-attack scenarios that isolates clarification as a distinct agent state and measures how this state transition affects vulnerability under controlled conditions. Each benchmark instance is evaluated under matched execution and clarification settings: in the execution setting, the agent acts on a fully specified instruction and encounters adversarial content only through tool-returned data; in the clarification setting, the agent must first request and incorporate additional user input before acting. We evaluate ten frontier LLMs and find that clarification-seeking consistently and substantially amplifies vulnerability. For instance, attack success rises from 1.8% to 34.0% for o3 and from 2.2% to 35.7% for Gemini-3-Flash. A decomposition analysis reveals that this gap reflects both a state-dependent shift in how models process incoming content and a channel-specific effect arising from the agent-solicited clarification interface. These findings demonstrate that standard execution-time security evaluation systematically underestimates the attack surface of interactive agents, and that robustness under fully specified tasks does not translate to robustness under ambiguity. For reproducibility, our data and source code are available at https://github.com/scaleapi/aspi.

</details>


### [211] [LPG: Balancing Efficiency and Policy Reasoning in Latent Policy Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17329)
*Nanxi Li,Zhengyue Zhao,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出潜策略护栏(LPG)框架，通过将动态策略的意图解读与政策依据的推理过程压缩为连续潜状态，实现低延迟、高精度的策略合规检测，LPG-4B以仅10个潜令牌达到84.5%安全准确率和77.9% F1，比最强动态基线快约11倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统中LLM作为定制化助手部署时，安全策略由用户、组织或监管环境在推理时动态指定，但现有护栏框架无法在不重新训练的情况下适应动态变化的策略，且同时满足策略推理所需的推理能力与低延迟响应之间存在根本性矛盾。

Method: LPG框架通过将意图解读和政策依据所需的内部分析过程压缩为连续潜状态，由决策相关语义进行监督。推理时仅生成简洁的裁决结果并锚定违反的策略条款，避免显式推理的延迟同时保持可审计性。

Result: 在策略护栏基准测试中，LPG-4B以仅10个潜令牌达到84.5%平均安全准确率和77.9% F1，超越最强动态基线，且单样本评估速度比Qwen3-4B-Thinking快约11倍。

Conclusion: LPG框架验证了通过潜空间压缩推理过程可以有效解决动态策略护栏中推理能力与低延迟之间的矛盾，实现高效且可审计的动态安全策略执行。

Abstract: Guardrails are a critical safety layer for modern AI systems, but their operating regime is changing. As LLMs are deployed as customized assistants, safety policies are increasingly specified at inference time by users, organizations, or regulatory contexts. This makes safety enforcement fundamentally dynamic: the guardrail should adapt to changing safety policies without retraining. Yet this requirement creates a fundamental tension: faithfully judging complex policy contexts demands reasoning capability, while practical deployment requires low-latency responses. We introduce Latent Policy Guardrail (LPG), a guardrail framework that learnssemantic latent deliberation over dynamic policies. LPG compresses the internal deliberation needed for intent interpretation and policy grounding into continuous states supervised by decision-relevant semantics. At inference time, it generates only a compact verdict anchored to the violated policy clauses, preserving auditability while avoiding the latency of explicit reasoning. Across policy guardrail benchmarks, LPG-4B reaches 84.5% average safety accuracy and 77.9% F1 by compressing deliberation into just 10 latent tokens, outperforming the strongest dynamic baseline while running roughly 11 times faster than Qwen3-4B-Thinking under the single-sample evaluation setup. Code and data are available at https://github.com/SaFo-Lab/Latent_Policy_Guard.

</details>


### [212] [Rethinking Side-Channel Analysis: Automated Discovery and Analysis of Side-Channel Leakage with LLM-Assisted Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17406)
*Zhen Xu,Zihao Wang,Yuhua Sun,XiaoFeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SCAgent是一个基于大语言模型和基础模型的自动化侧信道风险分析框架，能够在iOS平台上无需大量人工干预地发现敏感事件、系统性地探索侧信道，并通过少样本学习实现可扩展的隐私泄露分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有侧信道分析工作大多依赖手动定义目标事件和固定信道集合，缺乏自动化、系统化的分析方法，难以应对日益复杂的系统和应用场景。

Method: SCAgent采用LLM驱动的智能体进行系统探索以识别敏感目标；结合系统文档推理与显式验证来发现侧信道并缓解LLM幻觉；基于基础模型的少样本学习实现可扩展分析；引入时间移位鲁棒特征提取层以桥接时间序列信号与表格基础模型之间的鸿沟。

Result: 在iOS平台上，SCAgent在标准基准（前台应用识别、网站指纹识别）以及新发现的流行应用内敏感活动识别任务上均取得了有效结果。

Conclusion: SCAgent证明了大语言模型与基础模型在自动化侧信道风险分析中的可行性，为大规模、系统化的隐私风险评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: Side-channel attacks exploit unintended information leakage from system behavior and continue to pose serious privacy risks in modern platforms. Despite extensive prior work, side-channel analysis remains largely manual and fragmented, typically assuming predefined target events and a fixed set of known channels. As systems and applications grow increasingly complex, several fundamental questions remain unanswered: which user or system events are sensitive in practice, how side channels associated with these events can be systematically discovered without exhaustive manual effort, and how their leakage can be analyzed at scale without prohibitive data collection and model training costs. To address these questions, we present SCAgent, an automated framework for side-channel risk analysis. To identify sensitive targets beyond manually specified events, SCAgent performs agent-driven system exploration guided by LLM-based semantic reasoning. To systematically discover side channels while mitigating the risk of LLM hallucination, it reasons over system documentation and incorporates explicit verification to enforce semantic consistency, threat-model feasibility, and per-channel usability. To enable scalable analysis under limited data, SCAgent adopts a few-shot learning paradigm based on foundation models, avoiding the need to train bespoke models for each channel--event pair. To bridge the gap between raw time-series side-channel signals and tabular foundation models, SCAgent further introduces a time-shift--robust feature extraction layer that enables effective downstream analysis. We instantiate SCAgent on iOS as a first step, focusing on OS-level side channels observable by unprivileged applications. Our evaluation spans standard benchmarks such as foreground app and website fingerprinting, as well as newly identified sensitive in-app activities in popular applications.

</details>


### [213] [Trust No Tool: Evaluating and Defending LLM Agents under Untrusted Tool Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17453)
*Lecheng Yan,Ruizhe Li,Xicheng Han,Wenxi Li,Binwu Wang,Longyue Wang,Chenyang Lyu,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了工具型LLM智能体在依赖外部工具时面临的一种新型安全威胁——认知污染（cognitive poisoning），即恶意工具在探索阶段表现正常以积累信任，仅在隐藏状态条件与最终可执行动作对齐时才造成危害。为此构建了包含1,970个隐藏触发工具妥协片段的基准测试TRUST-Bench，提出了非对称惩罚指标GuardedJoint和轨迹感知的最终动作风险评分框架VISTA-Guard。实验表明，提示中心启发式、标量化特征和零样本评判在此场景下失效，而VISTA-Guard在域内和平衡域外评估中分别达到84.2和56.9的GuardedJoint分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体安全基准和防御方法隐含假设工具反馈在选定后是可信的，忽略了恶意工具通过良性反馈积累信任、仅在特定条件下触发攻击的认知污染威胁。

Method: 构建TRUST-Bench基准（1,970个隐藏触发工具妥协片段+匹配安全对照）；提出GuardedJoint非对称惩罚指标；设计VISTA-Guard框架，将多步工具交互抽象为结构化的环境变量以编码信任形成动态，基于轨迹条件化表示对最终可执行动作进行风险评分。

Result: 提示中心启发式、标量化特征和零样本评判在认知污染场景下失效。VISTA-Guard在域内评估GuardedJoint分数84.2，平衡域外评估56.9，而仅优化安全-效用单侧的方法分数归零。

Conclusion: 在黑盒工具生态系统中，关键防御目标不是局部提示文本或工具描述，而是跨交互轨迹形成的信任模式及最终动作中的承诺方式。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents increasingly rely on external tools to make consequential decisions, yet most existing agent-security benchmarks and defenses implicitly assume that tool feedback is trustworthy once a tool has been selected. We study a different failure mode, cognitive poisoning, in which a malicious tool behaves plausibly during exploration, accumulates trust through benign-looking feedback, and becomes harmful only when hidden state conditions align with the final executable action. To study this setting, we construct TRUST-Bench, a task-conditioned benchmark of 1,970 hidden-trigger tool-compromise episodes with matched safe controls, introduce an asymmetric penalty metric, GuardedJoint, to better reflect real deployment risk, and present VISTA-Guard, a backbone-agnostic framework for final-action risk scoring. The core idea is to abstract multi-step tool interaction into structured environment variables that encode trust-formation dynamics and then score the risk of the final executable action from this trajectory-conditioned representation. Experiments show that prompt-centric heuristics, scalarized features, and zero-shot judges fail in this regime, whereas trajectory-aware final-action scoring yields strong in-domain discrimination and remains effective under balanced out-of-distribution transfer. Under GuardedJoint, VISTA-Guard reaches $84.2$ in-domain and $56.9$ on balanced out-of-distribution evaluation, while methods that optimize only one side of the safety--utility tradeoff collapse to zero. These findings support a broader view of agent security in black-box tool ecosystems: the decisive defense target is not local prompt text or tool descriptors alone, but the way trust is formed across the interaction trajectory and committed through the final action.

</details>


### [214] [Explicit cost analysis of Toom-4 multiplication for incomplete NTT in lattice-based cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17505)
*Sakura Oku,Momonari Kudo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在非完整NTT框架下重新审视Toom-4乘法，给出了具体实现和显式操作计数，建立了基于加法链的简单成本模型，并分析了混合策略（Toom-4、Karatsuba和非完整NTT）的优势参数范围，通过实验验证了预测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非完整NTT虽放宽了对系数域模数的限制，但现有框架中Toom-4的精确成本尚未以兼容形式表达，缺乏统一的成本模型来指导混合策略的选择。

Method: 论文提出具体的Toom-4实现，推导出系数域上加法/减法与乘法的显式操作计数；基于加法链建立非完整NTT的简单成本模型；利用该模型分析Toom-4、Karatsuba和非完整NTT的混合策略。

Result: 确定了Toom-4具有优势的参数范围，并通过实验验证了成本模型的预测准确性。

Conclusion: Toom-4在非完整NTT框架下是有效的，所提出的成本模型能准确指导混合策略的选择，进一步优化格密码中的多项式乘法性能。

Abstract: Polynomial multiplication is fundamental in lattice-based cryptography. While the Number Theoretic Transform (NTT) enables fast multiplication, it imposes constraints on the modulus of the coefficient field. Hafiz et al. (2025) addressed this limitation by analyzing the incomplete NTT, which combines a truncated NTT with conventional multiplication methods In this work, we revisit Toom-4 multiplication in the context of incomplete NTT. Although Toom-4 is asymptotically faster than Karatsuba, its precise cost has not been expressed in a form compatible with the incomplete NTT framework. We present a concrete Toom-4 implementation and derive explicit operation counts that separate additions/subtractions and multiplications over the coefficient field. Our analysis based on addition chains yields a simple cost model for incomplete NTT. Using this model, we analyze hybrid strategies combining Toom-4, Karatsuba, and incomplete NTT. We identify parameter ranges where Toom-4 is advantageous and validate the predicted behavior experimentally.

</details>


### [215] [Speed Kills: Exploring Confused Deputy Attacks Through Edge AI Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17707)
*Datta Manikanta Sri Hari Danduri,Aravind Kumar Machiry*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次深入研究AI加速器（AIA）上的 confused deputy 攻击，提出LLM辅助框架DeputyHunt进行动静态分析，在7款主流AIA中发现6款存在漏洞，影响超1.28亿台设备，获CVE-2025-66425，并提出开销约15%的按需验证防御方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI加速器（AIA）在边缘/嵌入式设备中广泛部署，但由于不受操作系统限制且对应用处理器安全机制（如内核/应用内存隔离、进程隔离）可见性有限，存在语义鸿沟，可能导致 confused deputy 漏洞——恶意应用可诱骗AIA代为执行特权操作。目前尚无针对AIA的confused deputy攻击系统性研究。

Method: 设计DeputyHunt框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）辅助，结合动态分析与静态分析，提取给定AIA的confused deputy攻击相关信息。对Google、NVIDIA、Hailo、Texas Instruments、NXP、AWS、Rockchip七家厂商的AIA进行可行性分析。

Result: 7款AIA中有6款存在confused deputy攻击可行性，影响超过128款SoC和超过1亿台设备；获得CVE-2025-66425漏洞编号。提出的按需验证防御方案在Gem5-salam模拟器上评估，运行时开销约15%。

Conclusion: AIA存在严重的confused deputy安全风险，需引起业界重视；所提按需验证防御能以较低开销有效缓解该问题。

Abstract: AI Accelerator (AIA) are specialized hardware e.g., Tensor Processing Unit (TPU), that enable optimal and efficient execution of AI applications and on-device inference. The growing demand for AI applications has led to the widespread adoption of AIAs on Edge or embedded devices on Edge or embedded devices. Unlike applications, AIAs are not bound by Operating System (OS) restrictions and have limited visibility into Application Processor (AP) security mechanisms (e.g., kernel vs. application memory, process isolation). This semantic gap can lead to confused deputy vulnerabilities, i.e., AIA can be tricked by a malicious application to perform privileged operations on their behalf. In this paper, we conducted the first in-depth study of Confused Deputy Attacks (CDAs) using AIA. We design DeputyHunt, a Large Language Model (LLM) assisted framework to extract CDA relevant information for a given AIA through a combination of dynamic and static analysis. We used this information to explore the feasibility of CDA on seven different AIAs from popular vendors, i.e., Google, NVIDIA, Hailo, Texas Instruments, NXP, AWS, and Rockchip. Our analysis revealed that CDA is feasible on six out of the seven AIAs, impacting over 128 System On Chips (SOCs) and over 100 million devices. Our findings highlight critical security risks posed by AIA on system security. Our work has been acknowledged by the corresponding vendors and assigned the CVE-2025-66425. We propose an on-demand validation defense against CDA, and evaluation on the Gem5- salam simulator shows that it incurs minimal runtime overhead (i.e., ~15%).

</details>


### [216] [Explainable Machine Learning for Phishing Detection on Heterogeneous Datasets with MCP-Enabled Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17891)
*Nikhil Kumar Dora,Sumit Kumar Tetarave,Rishikesh Sahay,Madhusudan Singh,Xiaoqing Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究评估了多种机器学习算法（逻辑回归、CatBoost、CNN、DistilBERT）在钓鱼检测中的性能，并结合XAI技术（信息增益、SHAP、LIME）进行特征解释，最终提出了一个基于MCP的实时钓鱼URL检测系统。实验表明，DistilBERT以99.78%的准确率表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钓鱼攻击是数据泄露的主要成因之一（占36%），给用户和组织带来重大损失，亟需智能、自适应且可解释的安全检测机制。

Method: 在异构钓鱼数据集（UCI公开数据集、EvilGinx/Zphisher生成的数据集、AI生成数据集）上比较多种ML模型；采用信息增益、SHAP、LIME进行特征重要性分析；构建基于MCP协议的实时URL检测系统。

Result: 逻辑回归准确率92.44%、CatBoost 95.01%、CNN 94.02%、DistilBERT 99.78%。DistilBERT在变压器模型中表现最优。

Conclusion: Transformer模型（尤其是DistilBERT）在钓鱼检测任务上显著优于传统模型；结合XAI和MCP系统可实现高精度、可解释且可部署的实时检测方案。

Abstract: With the growth in digital transformation and Internet usage, the Social Engineering techniques such as Phishing have become a major concern for the users and the organizations. Phishing attacks involve deceptive techniques to trick users into revealing confidential information that causes financial loss and reputation damage to organizations. According to report of Verizon, 36% of all data breaches involved phishing, highlighting the need for intelligent, adaptive, and explainable security mechanisms. This paper examines the efficiency of different machine learning algorithms in phishing detection on heterogeneous phishing datasets that include a publicly available UCI dataset, our generated datasets using tools such as EvilGinx and Zphisher, and AI generated datasets. Moreover, this work incorporates explainable AI (XAI) techniques such as Information Gain, SHAP (SHapley Additive Explanations), and LIME (Local Interpretable Model-Agnostic Explanations) to examine the most influential features impacting classification outcomes. To support practical deployment, this work also incorporates an MCP-based phishing URL detection system that offers real-time URL analysis, feature extraction, confidence-based classification, and AI-assisted security interpretation. The experimental results demonstrate that among classical models the highest accuracy is obtained by Logistic Regression at 92.44%, among ensemble models CatBoost achieved the highest accuracy at 95.01%, among neural network CNN achieved an accuracy of 94.02%, and among transformer-based models, DistilBERT got the highest accuracy at 99.78%

</details>


### [217] [Operationalising Post Quantum TLS Automated Configuration Profiling and Hybrid PQC Deployment in Financial Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17955)
*Harish Balaji,Aarav Varshney,Prasanna Ravi,Sripal Jain,Robin Foe,Jorden Seet,Huaxiong Wang,Kwok-Yan Lam,Anupam Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动解析TLS加密配置的方法，用于帮助组织在异构环境中迁移至后量子密码（PQC）标准。该方法从主流企业Web服务器栈中提取并标准化TLS加密配置，生成统一的加密清单。作者在8,443个真实Nginx配置和一个金融机构的POC部署中验证了该方法，实现了MLKEM和混合MLKEM密钥交换的无缝部署，零应用层改动且性能开销可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机威胁迫在眉睫，组织需要升级加密基础设施至后量子安全。虽然PQC标准已存在，但实际迁移中操作层面的瓶颈远大于算法层面：安全团队缺乏对异构TLS终端点配置的精确可见性和可重复的迁移方法。

Method: 提出一种配置解析方法，自动从主流企业Web服务器栈（如Nginx）中提取并标准化TLS加密配置信息，生成带有来源追溯的统一加密清单，为迁移和合规提供基础。

Result: 在8,443个真实Nginx配置和一个金融机构的POC部署中验证成功，实现了MLKEM和混合MLKEM密钥交换在TLS终端点（Web服务器和API网关）的部署，零应用层改动且性能开销可控。

Conclusion: 后量子密码迁移的关键瓶颈在于操作层面而非算法层面，通过自动化的配置解析和清单生成方法，可以有效降低异构环境中的迁移复杂度，使组织能够在零应用层改动的前提下实现PQC兼容部署。

Abstract: Organisations are upgrading their cryptographic infrastructure to become quantum safe before large scale quantum computers materialise. Post quantum cryptography (PQC) standards now exist for key exchange and digital signatures, but the urgent question for adopters is how to operationalise PQC in complex environments with confidence. In banking, Transport Layer Security (TLS), for example, protects data in transit across public facing channels and internal services, and is terminated at many heterogeneous endpoints (web servers, API gateways, load balancers, reverse proxies), each a potential quantum vulnerable component and migration target. We argue that the bottleneck is operational rather than algorithmic, hybrid key exchanges such as MLKEM and hybrid MLKEM key exchanges are already available in mainstream libraries, but security teams lack precise visibility into TLS configurations and repeatable methods for enabling PQC compatible settings across a heterogeneous estate. This paper presents a configuration parsing methodology that automatically extracts and normalises TLS cryptographic posture across dominant enterprise web server stacks, producing a unified, provenance traced cryptographic inventory as a foundation for migration and compliance. We demonstrate the approach on 8,443 real world Nginx configurations from public repositories and in a proof of concept deployment at a financial institution, where MLKEM and hybrid MLKEM key exchanges at TLS termination points (web server and API gateway) securing an internal application, with zero application layer changes and manageable performance overhead.

</details>


### [218] [Babel: Jailbreaking Safety Attention via Obfuscation Distribution Optimized Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17971)
*Ziwei Wang,Jing Chen,Ruichao Liang,Zhi Wang,Yebo Feng,Ju Jia,Ruiying Du,Cong Wu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM的安全对齐依赖于少量稀疏分布的注意力头，导致表示空间大部分区域监控薄弱。基于此提出Babel攻击框架，通过系统化混淆采样和迭代反馈分布优化，在仅40次查询内对GPT-4o和Claude-3-5-haiku分别实现82.67%和78.33%的攻击成功率，达到SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒越狱方法依赖启发式模板或穷举尝试，缺乏机制可解释性和查询效率，亟需理解LLM安全机制的内在漏洞并设计高效攻击方法。

Method: 首先形式化描述安全对齐仅依赖少量稀疏分布注意力头的脆弱性，建立数学越狱模型刻画文本混淆的有效边界。在此基础上提出Babel框架，通过系统化混淆采样结合迭代反馈驱动的分布优化，在黑盒条件下实现高效可靠的越狱攻击。

Result: 在GPT-4o上ASR从41.33%提升至82.67%，在Claude-3-5-haiku上从38.33%提升至78.33%，平均仅需40次查询，查询效率和攻击成功率均达SOTA。

Conclusion: 揭示了LLM安全机制因注意力头分布稀疏而存在固有脆弱性，Babel提供了一种可解释、高效的黑盒红队测试方法，为LLM安全研究提供了新的评估工具和理论视角。

Abstract: Despite rigorous safety alignment, Large Language Models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks. Existing black-box methods often rely on heuristic templates or exhaustive trials, lacking mechanistic interpretability and query efficiency. In this study, we investigate an intrinsic vulnerability in the safety mechanisms of LLMs, where safety alignment relies on a small set of sparsely distributed attention heads, leaving much of the representational space weakly monitored. We formalize this phenomenon with a mathematical jailbreaking model that characterizes the delicate boundary of effective text obfuscation and analytically explains observed jailbreak behaviors. Guided by this model, we propose Babel, an efficient black-box attack framework that exploits the identified safety gap through systematic obfuscation sampling with iterative, feedback-driven distribution refinement, enabling reliable and high-success jailbreak attacks without access to model internals. Comprehensive evaluations on frontier commercial models demonstrate that Babel achieves state-of-the-art attack success rates and superior query efficiency. Specifically, compared to state-of-the-art methods, Babel increases the attack success rate on GPT-4o from 41.33% to 82.67% and on Claude-3-5-haiku from 38.33% to 78.33% within an average of 40 queries, providing a robust red-teaming methodology for LLMs safety research.

</details>


### [219] [DARTIC: Decentralized Anonymous Reputation at Scale for Trustworthy Crowdsourcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18146)
*Mouhamed Amine Bouchiha,Mourad Rabah,Ronan Champagnat,Abdelaziz Amara Korba,Yacine Ghamri-Doudane*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DARTIC，一个去中心化、匿名且可扩展的声誉驱动众包框架，通过双账本系统、zkSNARK零知识证明和聚合技术，同时实现了匿名性、声誉绑定与可扩展性，实验表明在众感知和联邦学习场景下性能优良。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有链上众包的去中心化声誉框架无法同时满足匿名性、声誉绑定和可扩展性这三个需求，本文旨在在最小信任模型下同时实现这些目标。

Method: 提出DARTIC框架，包含：(1) 双账本系统，允许用户在不同交互中使用不同假名，实现不可链接性同时保持可问责性；(2) zkSNARK集合成员证明，将所有用户假名加密绑定到单一访问令牌以抵御女巫攻击和声誉重置攻击；(3) 两种聚合技术将多个证明压缩为单个简洁证明以减少验证开销；(4) 自动化隐私保护声誉模型，动态评估不同众包场景下的贡献。

Result: (1) 单一代币花费的证明生成时间小于3秒；(2) 聚合技术将1024个证明的验证时间从8.7秒降至0.96秒；(3) zk-batching相较纯Layer-1部署降低gas成本超过100倍。

Conclusion: 匿名性、鲁棒的声誉绑定和可扩展性可以在完全去中心化的众包系统中同时实现。

Abstract: On-chain crowdsourcing leverages blockchain's decentralization, transparency, and tamper-resistance to build trustworthy and verifiable Web3 crowdsourced services. However, existing decentralized reputation frameworks do not reconcile anonymity, reputation binding, and scalability. This paper demonstrates how on-chain crowdsourcing can simultaneously achieve these requirements under a trust-minimized model. We introduce DARTIC, a decentralized, anonymous, and scalable reputation-driven framework for crowdsourcing. DARTIC presents a dual-ledger system that enables requesters and workers to use distinct pseudonyms across interactions, ensuring unlinkability while maintaining accountability. To mitigate Sybil and reputation-reset attacks, we employ zkSNARK-based set membership proofs, cryptographically binding all user pseudonyms to a single access token without revealing the linkage. For scalability, we investigate two aggregation techniques that compress multiple proofs into a single succinct proof to minimize verification overhead. In addition, we design an automated, privacy-preserving reputation model that dynamically evaluates contributions across diverse crowdsourcing contexts. To demonstrate practicality, we instantiate and assess DARTIC in both crowdsensing and federated learning scenarios. Experimental results show that (i) individual proof generation for token spending completes in less than 3s, (ii) aggregation reduces the verification time of 1024 proofs from 8.7s to 0.96s, and (iii) zk-batching lowers gas costs by more than 100x compared to a pure Layer-1 deployment. These results demonstrate that anonymity, robust reputation binding, and scalability can be jointly achieved in fully decentralized crowdsourcing systems.

</details>


### [220] [Acoustic Interference: A New Paradigm Weaponizing Acoustic Latent Semantic for Universal Jailbreak against Large Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18168)
*Yanyun Wang,Yu Huang,Zi Liang,Xixin Wu,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出声学干扰攻击（AIA），揭示大型音频语言模型（LALM）的安全对齐可仅通过声学潜在语义（ALS）被破坏。AIA将音频角色从恶意内容载体转变为安全对齐干扰器，使用通用、指令无关的干扰音频使文本查询绕过安全机制，在10个LALM和5个数据集上达到最先进的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对LALM的越狱攻击方法将音频视为恶意载荷的载体，依赖语义优化、声学参数控制或加性扰动将有害内容嵌入音频信号。本文质疑这种必要性，提出音频角色应从内容注入转变为安全对齐干扰，探索仅通过音频的内在副语言特征即可破坏LALM安全对齐的可能性。

Method: 提出声学干扰攻击（AIA），将攻击载荷与音频解耦。AIA使用一组通用的、指令无关的干扰音频，这些音频内容无害但注入特定的声学潜在语义（ALS），使标准恶意文本查询能够绕过安全对齐，无需针对特定实例进行优化。

Result: 在10个LALM和5个数据集上的广泛实验表明，AIA实现了最先进的攻击成功率。可解释性分析揭示了AIA导致的推理路径漂移，并识别了ALS中固有的有效模式，暴露了LALM跨模态对齐的根本脆弱性。

Conclusion: 本文证明了音频模态对LALM安全对齐的威胁不仅限于内容注入，声学潜在语义本身即可作为通用越狱触发器。这一发现揭示了跨模态对齐的根本脆弱性，为LALM安全防护提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The integration of audio modality into Large Audio Language Models (LALMs) significantly expands their attack surface. Existing jailbreak paradigms predominantly treat audio as a carrier for malicious payloads, relying on semantic optimization, acoustic parameter control, or additive perturbation to embed harmful content into the audio signal. In this work, we challenge this necessity and propose a new paradigm in which the role of audio shifts from content injection to safety alignment interference. We reveal that LALM safety alignment can be compromised solely by specific Acoustic Latent Semantics (ALS), the underlying paralinguistic features intrinsic to the priors of audio generative models. Distinct from previous works that leverage explicit acoustic parameters to merely style malicious audio, we demonstrate that interference audio, benign in content but infused with specific ALS, can serve as a universal jailbreak trigger. Leveraging this insight, we propose the Acoustic Interference Attack (AIA), which decouples the attack payload from the audio. Specifically, AIA employs a set of universal, instruction-neutral interference audio, enabling standard malicious text queries to bypass safety alignment without instance-specific optimization. Extensive experiments on 10 LALMs across five datasets demonstrate that AIA achieves the state-of-the-art attack success rate. Furthermore, our interpretability analysis uncovers the inference path drift induced by AIA and identifies the inherent effective patterns within ALS, revealing the fundamental vulnerability of cross-modal alignment in LALMs.

</details>


### [221] [Prompt2Fingerprint: Plug-and-Play LLM Fingerprinting via Text-to-Weight Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18474)
*Sixu Chen,Xiang Chen,Hongyao Yu,Jiaxin Hong,Hao Fang,Shuoyang Sun,Bin Chen,Shu-Tao Xia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Prompt2Fingerprint (P2F)框架，将LLM指纹注入从独立的优化任务重构为条件参数生成任务，通过生成器将文本描述直接映射为低秩参数增量，实现即插即用的指纹注入，在保持高精度和鲁棒性的同时大幅降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM指纹方法（特别是主动方法）需要为每个新身份进行独立的、资源密集型的微调训练，存在严重的可扩展性瓶颈，计算成本高昂且部署延迟大。

Method: 将指纹注入重新定义为条件参数生成任务，设计专用生成器将文本描述直接映射为低秩参数增量（LoRA风格），只需一次前向传播即可完成指纹注入，无需额外的模型重训练。

Result: P2F在保持高指纹准确率、无害性和鲁棒性的同时，显著降低了计算开销，实现了可扩展且即时的LLM所有权管理方案。

Conclusion: P2F是首个将指纹注入转化为条件参数生成任务的框架，通过即插即用设计解决了现有方法可扩展性差的痛点，为大规模LLM部署中的所有权追踪提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: The widespread deployment and redistribution of large language models (LLMs) have made model provenance tracking a critical challenge. While existing LLM fingerprinting methods, particularly active approaches that embed identity signals via fine-tuning, achieve high accuracy and robustness, they suffer from significant scalability bottlenecks. These methods typically treat fingerprint injection as an independent, one-off optimization task rather than a reusable capability, necessitating separate, resource-intensive training for every new identity. This incurs prohibitive computational costs and deployment delays. To address this, we propose Prompt2Fingerprint (P2F), the first framework that reformulates fingerprinting as a conditional parameter generation task. By leveraging a specialized generator, P2F maps textual descriptions directly to low-rank parameter increments in a single forward pass, enabling plug-and-play LLM fingerprint injection without further model retraining. Our experiments demonstrate that P2F maintains high fingerprint accuracy, harmlessness, and robustness while significantly reducing computational overhead, offering a scalable and instant solution for LLM ownership management.

</details>


### [222] [Not What You Asked For: Typographic Attacks in Household Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18593)
*Ali Iranmanesh,Peng Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在家庭机器人操控的完整感知-规划-执行流水线中，物理场景中印刷文本（typographic attacks）对视觉语言模型（如CLIP）的对抗性攻击效果。在Habitat仿真环境中，攻击成功率达67.8%，且感知错误会通过3D语义地图传播，导致机器人实际抓取并运送错误物体，表明这是一种真实且可测量的物理安全威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作仅在静态2D基准和3D导航任务中量化了typographic攻击的威胁，但未探索其对家庭机器人完整操控流水线（感知-规划-执行）的影响。

Method: 在Habitat仿真中使用HomeRobot基准，设计了解耦感知架构：暴露冻结的CLIP编码器以接收对抗性贴纸，同时通过DETIC保持几何定位。在59个可归因情节的受控评估池中，未经视角优化和遮挡优化的条件下进行攻击测试。

Result: 攻击总体成功率为67.8%，完全成功情节中达70.0%。感知错误通过持久3D语义地图传播，导致运动性失败——机器人实际抓取并运送错误物体到目标容器。

Conclusion: Typographic误分类对模块化操控流水线构成了真实、可测量且具有物理后果的安全威胁，这是以往typographic攻击研究未涉及的领域。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary embodied AI agents increasingly rely on vision-language models such as CLIP for object perception and task grounding. However, the shared embedding space that enables this flexibility introduces a structural vulnerability to typographic attacks, where printed text in a physical scene semantically overrides visual judgment. While prior work has quantified this threat in static 2D benchmarks and 3D navigation tasks, its impact on the full Sense-Plan-Act pipeline of household robot manipulation remains unexplored.
  This work evaluates typographic attacks in a Habitat-based simulation using the HomeRobot benchmark. We introduce a decoupled perception architecture that exposes a frozen CLIP encoder to adversarial stickers while maintaining geometric grounding via DETIC. In a controlled evaluation pool of 59 attributable episodes, the attack achieves an overall Attack Success Rate (ASR) of 67.8%, rising to 70.0% among fully successful episodes, under uncontrolled viewing angles and occlusion with no perceptual optimization.
  Critically, we find that perceptual errors propagate through the persistent 3D semantic map to produce kinetic failures, defined here as physically executed grasping and transport of the wrong object driven by an adversarially poisoned semantic state. In these cases, the robot physically grasps and delivers the wrong object to a target receptacle. These results establish typographic misclassification as a real, measurable, and physically consequential threat to the safety of modular manipulation pipelines that prior typographic attack research has left unexamined.

</details>


### [223] [Sublinear Risk-Limiting Audits from Direct Ballot Selection and Statistical Ballot Manifests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18670)
*Benjamin Fuller,Abigail Harrison,Alexander Russell*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出两种新型风险限制审计（RLA）技术：1）统计机制从粗略选票清单自举出精确清单，亚线性工作代价；2）直接选票选择法，反向比较随机选票与其投票记录，支持无序标识符选举。实验表明，清单创建时间可大幅降低（整体审计时间至少减少一个数量级），直接选票选择法在较小票数差距下优于现有方法（1%差距比Minerva提升55%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的风险限制审计（RLA）要求精确的选票批次清单（ballot manifest），而选举程序通常只能提供粗略估计，即使微小误差（与审计差距相当）也会使传统RLA完全失效。建立足够精确的清单需要处理每张选票，是RLA的主要成本瓶颈。因此需要降低清单精确性要求或减少清单构建成本的方法。

Method: 1）统计自举机制：利用不可信制表系统报告的批次规模，通过统计检验从粗略清单自举出精确清单，仅需亚线性工作量。2）直接选票选择法（Direct Ballot Selection）：反向传统比较流程，随机选择选票与其投票记录（CVR）比较，设计针对标识符重复的统计检验，支持无序标识符选举。

Result: 1）清单创建时间大幅降低，3%票数差距和大规模人口下，整体审计时间至少减少一个数量级。2）直接选票选择法在较小票数差距下优于现有方法：在1%差距下（以康涅狄格州为基准），比Minerva（Security 2022）在选票样本复杂度上提升55%。

Conclusion: 所提两种技术显著降低了多种选举场景下RLA的复杂度。统计自举机制以少量额外抽样换取大幅降低清单创建时间；直接选票选择法在紧密选举中表现优异。

Abstract: Risk-limiting audits (RLAs) are post-election auditing procedures that rigorously guarantee a specified maximum probability that an incorrect electoral outcome will not be detected. Aside from ready access to physical ballots, known RLAs require a software-independent accounting of the sizes of each ballot batch, called a ballot manifest. While typical electoral procedures automatically provide rough estimates for batch sizes, even slight inaccuracies (commensurate with the margin of the contest under audit) completely invalidate conventional RLAs (Lindeman et al., EVT 2012). Thus, establishing a sufficiently accurate manifest often requires handling every ballot and can be the dominant cost of conducting the RLA.
  We propose two new risk-limiting techniques: 1) A statistical mechanism for ensuring that the batch sizes reported by an untrusted tabulation are, in fact, an accurate manifest; this effectively bootstraps from a rough manifest to an accurate one with sublinear effort. 2) We propose a new class of RLAs called direct ballot selection. This method reverses the traditional comparison procedure and compares uniformly selected ballots against their cast vote records, requiring a new statistical test for identifier duplication but efficiently supporting elections without in order identifiers.
  These techniques reduce the complexity of RLAs across many elections. Our two main findings are as follows: 1) The time to create a manifest can be drastically reduced with a modest increase in the number of ballots sampled in the audit. At a 3% margin and a large population, there is a reduction in the overall audit time of at least an order of magnitude across methods. 2) Direct ballot selection improves over state-of-the-art polling for small margins. For Connecticut (29th in population) at a 1% margin, it beats Minerva (Security 2022) by 55% in ballot sample complexity.

</details>
